## Request

P0: TUI sidebar status section for `project_message` inbox + outbox, showing UNRESOLVED
messages, modeled on AFT and magic-context sidebars, INCLUDING a click toggle to
expand/collapse the section.

P1: reload/modify project-message connections without reloading either OpenCode session.

## Resolved forks (user answers, §2058§)

- Q1 OUTBOX semantics → TWO states only: (1) sent, (2) read-by-dest. "Read" = the
  destination's idle-drain ACK (picked up by the idle session), NOT task completion.
- Q2 TOGGLE → "look at how magic-context does it" (repo: /Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/magic-context).
  Replicate MC's pattern: ▶/▼ triangle header, click row to toggle, persist collapse.
- Q3 RELOAD → option 1 (lazy re-read per operation). PLUS fix the enabled-flip problem by
  changing the schema DEFAULT `enabled` false→true so the tool+hook always register;
  user emulates "off" via no permissions (default_sender_access: allow-none + empty senders).
- TEST STRATEGY → TDD; project NOT done until a LIVE test is completed with evidence
  (drive the real opencode harness via opencode-qa skill; evidence under .omo/evidence/).

## Grounding — verified mechanisms (paths)

### Q1 ack mechanism (CONFIRMED)
- `MailboxStore.ack(messageId)` (mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:116) renames the note from the
  inbox (or `.delivering-<id>.md` reserved) to
  `<targetRepoRoot>/coordination_notes/<fromProjectId>/processed/<id>.md`.
- The idle-drain hook does NOT call ack() at dispatch — it `reserve()`s + `markDispatched()`s
  (idle-drain-hook.ts:119,136). ack() runs later via `reclaimStale`→`reclaimOne` once the
  injected message is confirmed in session history (state history_confirmed). So
  "read-by-dest" ≈ note has reached `processed/` in the TARGET repo.
- SENDER detects it: sender knows the target repoRoot via the registry. For each outbox
  entry (toProjectId, messageId), test existence of
  `<targetRepoRoot>/coordination_notes/<thisProjectId>/processed/<messageId>.md`.
  Exists → "read"; else → "sent" (unresolved). This is the new resolution C1 must add.
- CURRENT reader (`sidebar/mailbox-sidebar.ts`) only counts INBOUND unread/processed for
  THIS repo + a recentSent COUNT from the outbox log. It does NOT cross-reference target
  processed/ for outbound ack, and takes only (repoRoot, config) — no registry. C1 must
  inject the registry (listProjects → repoRoot map) and add outbound ack resolution.
- Outbox log: `send-tool/outbox-log.ts` appends JSON lines (OutboxEntry: sentAt,
  toProjectId, messageId, intent, correlationId, body). readRecentSent parses it.

### Q2 toggle pattern (CONFIRMED feasible; MC reference read in full)
- MC `sidebar-content.tsx`: slot-factory closure (plugin lifetime) holds a durable
  `SidebarController` (createSidebarController) owning prefs/collapsed/toggleCollapsed
  signals + a shared file watcher. Header box has
  `onMouseDown={() => controller.toggleCollapsed()}`, label `▶ `/`▼ ` triangle.
  Persists collapse via `queueTuiPreferenceUpdate(PLUGIN_KEY=["magic-context"],
  ["collapsed"], next)` to `~/.config/opencode/tui-preferences.jsonc` (comment-json,
  atomic temp+rename). => The host DOES forward mouse events into sidebar_content slot
  content. Click-toggle is real, no keybind fallback needed.
- Shared cross-plugin convention (tui-preferences.ts): anthropic-auth/AFT/MC all share
  ONE file `tui-preferences.jsonc`, one top-level key per plugin, byte-identical
  computeEffectiveOrder, default-order ladder (anthropic-auth 160, AFT 180, MC 200).
  omo can adopt the same file with key "oh-my-openagent" for collapse persistence.

### omo sidebar render model (DIFFERENT from MC — adapt, don't copy JSX)
- `tui.ts`: imperative `@opentui/solid` runtime. `materialize(buildViewNodes(currentView,
  theme), solid)` via solid.createElement/setProp/insert. Poll loop (POLL_INTERVAL_MS,
  constants.ts) → readView → viewKey diff → api.renderer.requestRender(). requestRender
  re-invokes renderSidebar (this is the ONLY update path today, so it's proven to re-run).
- `ViewNode` (element-helpers.ts) = { kind:"box"|"text", props:Record<string,unknown>,
  text?, children? }. props pass through setProp → an `onMouseDown` FUNCTION prop threads
  cleanly onto a box node.
- `buildViewNodes` (render-view.ts) is a PURE switch over view.kind (active/broken/idle).
  It has NO mailbox case today. `computeView`/`viewKey` (compute-view.ts) ALREADY thread
  `mailbox?` + hash `stableMailboxKey` (inboundUnread, inboundProcessed, recentSentCount)
  — but `readView()` in tui.ts NEVER populates sections.mailbox, so it's always absent.
- Collapse state is LOCAL UI state, NOT on disk → it must live in a tui.ts closure var
  (mirroring MC's controller), threaded into buildViewNodes as a param; the onMouseDown
  handler flips it + calls api.renderer.requestRender(). It is intentionally NOT part of
  viewKey (poll diff tracks data; toggle drives its own render).

### C4 config-freeze points (the stale surface for P1)
- Tool: `createMailboxToolsRecord` (tool-registry-mailbox-tools.ts) gates on
  `config?.enabled`, captures `config` + `createProjectRegistry()` ONCE. The tool's
  execute() closes over deps.config. Registry is re-read per call (listProjects), but
  config is frozen. Lazy re-read = execute() re-reads project config from disk each call.
- Hook: `createMailboxSessionHooks` captures config once → createMailboxHooks(ctx, config)
  → idle-drain deps.config frozen. Lazy re-read = the session.idle handler re-reads fresh
  config each drain.
- enabled is the ONE field not hot-reloadable via lazy re-read alone, because tool/hook
  REGISTRATION is session-start gated. Q3 fix removes the problem: default enabled=true so
  registration always happens; all real gating (senders/budget/bounds) is lazy-reloadable.

### auto-provision (auto-provision.ts) — must change
- Writes a stub `.opencode/oh-my-openagent.jsonc` with `enabled:false`,
  default_sender_access allow-none, empty senders. With the Q3 default flip this stub
  should write `enabled:true` (or omit enabled and rely on the new default) so a freshly
  provisioned project is on-but-permissionless ("emulated off"). NOTE (risk, out of
  scope): LOCAL_SCHEMA_PATH is a hardcoded agent-harness absolute path — portability smell.

## Topology lock (components)

- C1 — Sidebar state reader: inbound unresolved + outbound unresolved (sent vs ACKed via
  target processed/). Inject registry; add outbound-ack resolution.
- C2 — Render integration: populate sections.mailbox in readView; add a mailbox section to
  buildViewNodes (the orphaned-Todo-11 wiring, finished).
- C3 — Interactive expand/collapse toggle: ▶/▼ header, onMouseDown → flip closure flag +
  requestRender; persist collapse to tui-preferences.jsonc under key "oh-my-openagent".
- C4 — Config hot-reload (P1): lazy re-read project config per send + per drain; flip

  allow-none + empty senders.

## Defaults adopted (not asked — evidence-backed)

- Collapse persistence file: shared `~/.config/opencode/tui-preferences.jsonc`, key
  "oh-my-openagent" (matches the established cross-plugin convention MC/AFT use).
- Sidebar shows only when there's signal: if enabled but inbound+outbound unresolved all
  zero, render a single muted "Mailbox idle" line (collapsed-equivalent) rather than a big
  empty box — avoids noise now that enabled defaults true everywhere.
- Sidebar slot stays in the existing omo registration (order 900); mailbox is a SECTION
  within omo's sidebar, not a separate slot.

# ROADMAP

- [What This Is](#what-this-is)
- [Current Priority: Package Layering Refactor](#current-priority-package-layering-refactor)
- [Architecture Direction](#architecture-direction)
- [Multi-Harness Support (Exploratory)](#multi-harness-support-exploratory)
- [Why Not OpenCode-Native](#why-not-opencode-native)
- [Non-Goals](#non-goals)
- [Decision Principle](#decision-principle)

## What This Is

Oh-my-opencode is a harness for agents.

The human is not the worker. The agent is the worker. The human says what they want. Then they leave. The agent does the work. The human does not come back to fix details. The human does not come back to clarify. The human does not come back at all.

OMO does not make agents better at small tasks. OMO makes it possible to hand off big tasks. The kind of tasks where a human would normally stay in the loop for hours. OMO removes that loop.

The agent thinks. The agent decides. The agent executes. The human only initiates.

## Current Priority: Package Layering Refactor

**This is the most urgent work.**

The current `packages/` directory mixes binaries, web apps, MCP servers, and pure TypeScript logic in one flat namespace. This makes reuse across harnesses impossible and creates duplication across three repositories (`omo`, `pi-extensions`, `codex-plugins`).

The refactor splits packages into strict layers by runtime boundary:

| Layer | Contents | Boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Core | Pure TypeScript logic: rule discovery, AGENTS.md parsing, config schemas, model capabilities, todo state machines | No harness dependencies. Testable in isolation. |
| MCP | External tool servers: LSP, ast-grep | stdio process boundary. Host-agnostic. |
| Skills | Static declarative files (SKILL.md) | Markdown consumed by the agent. No code. |
| Adapters | Harness-specific glue: OpenCode plugin, Pi extensions, Codex plugins | Thin wrappers. Import core, wrap in harness API, export. |
| Platform | Bun compile binaries per target | Deployment artifacts. Never imported. |
| Web | Marketing site | Independent application. |

**Dependency rule:** The DAG flows downward only. Adapters depend on Core, MCP, and Skills. Nothing depends on Adapters. Platform and Web are leaves.

**Migration principle:** Existing behavior is preserved. Nothing breaks. Each extraction is a pure move: copy logic into Core, make the original location re-export from Core, verify tests still pass, then delete the duplicate in the other repositories.

**Current extraction status:**

- 18 Core packages are now extracted under `packages/`: `utils`, `model-core`, `prompts-core`, `rules-engine`, `agents-md-core`, `comment-checker-core`, `hashline-core`, `boulder-state`, `telemetry-core`, `lsp-core`, `mcp-stdio-core`, `tmux-core`, `claude-code-compat-core`, `skills-loader-core`, `mcp-client-core`, `openclaw-core`, `team-core`, and `delegate-core`.
- `omo` consumes these packages via workspace dependencies, with adapter shims left at original `packages/omo-opencode/src/` locations where OpenCode-facing import paths or runtime wiring still need stable anchors.
- `pi-extensions` and `codex-plugins` are not yet migrated to consume these packages. That migration is the next phase.
- The `lsp-tools-mcp` and `lsp-daemon` packages are vendored in-tree and now consume `lsp-core` plus `mcp-stdio-core` instead of deep-importing each other's source internals.

Layering achieved: Core (19 pure-TS packages) → MCP packages → Adapters (`omo` OpenCode plugin and Codex Light) → Platform binaries. Future Pi and external Codex repositories can consume the same Core layer.

The Pi Engine DI abstraction was deferred. It can be revisited once the adapter migration is complete.

## Architecture Direction

The codebase is built for the agent doing the work, not for the human reading it. If a structure is harder for a human to understand but makes the agent's job easier, we keep it. If a pattern adds friction to the agent's reasoning, we remove it.

The hierarchy of expression is:

1. **Skill** (static knowledge, zero runtime cost)
2. **MCP** (external tool with process boundary)
3. **Tool** (first-party runtime capability)
4. **Hook** (injection into the agent loop itself)

This order is not dogma. If the loop performs better another way, we change it. Agent performance is the only metric.

## Multi-Harness Support (Exploratory)

We may support additional harnesses: Claude Code, Codex, Pi, Amp, Droid, and others. Not confirmed. The current codebase is strongly coupled to OpenCode. Extracting the pure logic into a harness-neutral layer is a prerequisite if we ever do this.

Most harnesses share common lifecycle hooks: pre-tool-use guards, post-tool-use transforms, system message injection, model parameter overrides. One could abstract these into a unified hook layer. Rule injection could become a harness-agnostic primitive that adapts to each plugin API.

We are skeptical of this abstraction.

The industry changes too fast. Fixed patterns and agreed conventions should be implemented directly. Uncertain parts should not be over-abstracted. If an adapter for a new harness is needed, an agent can write it in one shot. The connection points are a single question away. Premature "adapter pattern" abstraction across unstable interfaces causes more pain than duplication.

We express what each component does in markdown documentation, not in interface definitions.

## Why Not OpenCode-Native

OpenCode is the current host. But its plugin API makes it trivial to break the main agent loop.

Session prompt injection (`session.prompt`, `session.promptAsync`) returns before the prompt is durably accepted. Later failures arrive as `session.error`. Multiple hooks observe the same idle or error edge and inject the same internal message into a live parent session. Duplicate work. Infinite loops. State corruption.

The TUI burns CPU.

Breaking changes are frequent.

These are not OpenCode-specific flaws. Any plugin system that exposes the main loop to arbitrary injection has the same disease. We treat OpenCode as one adapter target among several. Not the center of the architecture.

## Non-Goals

- We will not create a grand unified plugin interface that abstracts every harness.
- We will not prioritize human-readable file organization over agent loop performance.
- We will not fill in unspecified human details as a primary objective. The harness completes what was stated, using the model's natural representation.

## Decision Principle

When in doubt, prefer the representation that requires the least reasoning from the agent doing the work.

If that makes the directory structure messy for a human, the directory structure is wrong for humans and right for agents.

# mailbox-tui-sidebar-and-hot-reload - Work Plan

## TL;DR (For humans)
<!-- Fill this LAST, after the detailed plan below is written, so it summarizes the REAL plan. -->
<!-- Plain English for a non-engineer: NO file paths, NO todo numbers, NO wave/agent/tool names. -->

**What you'll get:** Your terminal sidebar gains a Mailbox panel that shows, at a glance, how many cross-project notes are waiting for you to read and how many notes you sent are still unread by the other project — and you can click its header to fold it away, just like the Magic Context and AFT panels. Separately, you can change which projects may message you (or how much they may ask for) by editing one config file, and both sessions pick up the change on their next message or idle moment — no restart.

**Why this approach:** Cross-project messaging becomes always-available (the tool and the background pickup are always wired in), and you turn a project "off" simply by granting it no permissions, which removes the old need to restart to flip an on/off switch. "Read by the other project" is detected from the acknowledgement the receiving project already writes to disk when its idle session picks the note up.

**What it will NOT do:** It will not interrupt a session you are actively typing in (notes still wait for an idle moment). It will not track whether the other project finished the work — only whether it read the note. It will not watch the config file in the background; it re-reads it the next time it sends or goes idle.

**Effort:** Medium
**Risk:** Medium - flipping cross-project messaging to on-by-default changes behavior for every project that uses this fork; mitigated by a permissionless "off" state and a cheap no-op when a project has no senders.
**Decisions to sanity-check:** on-by-default for all projects (off = no permissions); "read" means picked-up-by-idle, not task-done; rejected notes shown as a distinct failed state; collapse state shared in the same preferences file AFT/Magic Context use.

Your next move: approve, or run a high-accuracy Momus + Oracle review first. Full execution detail follows below.

---

> TL;DR (machine): Medium effort, Medium risk. 10 todos / 3 waves + 4-check final wave. Finish orphaned mailbox-sidebar wiring (reader→readView→buildViewNodes), add ▶/▼ click-toggle persisted to tui-preferences.jsonc, add 3-state outbound ack (processed=read / rejected=failed / neither=sent), and lazy per-operation config re-read with schema default `enabled`→true (block defaults populated). TDD + mandatory live opencode-qa evidence.

## Scope
### Must have
- A Mailbox section in omo's TUI sidebar showing UNRESOLVED inbound (notes others sent me, not yet drained) and UNRESOLVED outbound (notes I sent, not yet read by the destination).
- Outbound resolution is 3-state per sent note: **read** (destination wrote it to its `processed/`), **failed** (destination wrote it to its `rejected/`), **sent/unresolved** (neither yet). "Unresolved outbound" = sent-not-yet-read-or-failed.
- A ▶/▼ click toggle on the Mailbox header (opentui `onMouseDown`) that expands/collapses the section, mirroring Magic Context / AFT; collapse state persists across restarts in the shared `~/.config/opencode/tui-preferences.jsonc` under top-level key `"oh-my-openagent"`.
- The section renders in BOTH the `active` and `idle` sidebar views (a project can have mailbox signal with no agents/jobs/loop running).
- P1: editing a project's `.opencode/oh-my-openagent.jsonc` `cross_project_mailbox` (senders, default_sender_access, intent budgets, bounds) takes effect WITHOUT restarting either session — the `project_message` send and the idle-drain both re-read the merged config per operation.
- Schema fix so `enabled` no longer needs a mid-session flip: the whole `cross_project_mailbox` block DEFAULTS to a populated object with `enabled: true`, so the tool + idle-drain always register; "off" is emulated by `default_sender_access: "allow-none"` + empty `senders`. An explicit `"enabled": false` remains an honored hard-off (no registration).
- TDD for every code todo; a LIVE opencode-qa harness run proving the sidebar, the toggle, the sent→read transition, and a no-restart permission change, with evidence under `.omo/evidence/<date>-mailbox-tui-hot-reload/`.

### Must NOT have (guardrails, anti-slop, scope boundaries)
- NO `allow-interrupt` / preemption of an active session — idle-drain delivery semantics are unchanged.
- NO `fs.watch` on the config — reload is lazy per-operation only (the chosen Q3 option).
- NO bespoke single-file JSONC parse for the hot-reload — it MUST reuse the existing merged-config loader (`validatePluginConfig` / `loadPluginConfig`) so user-config + walked project configs + legacy basenames + merge semantics are preserved.
- NO unbounded outbox scan — outbound ack resolution is limited to a bounded recent window of sent entries.
- NO raw `session.promptAsync` — the idle-drain stays on `dispatchInternalPrompt`; the prompt-async-route-audit must stay green.
- NO change to the registry hot-path (it is already re-read per send/drain).
- NO emojis, no AI-slop comments, no `as any`/`@ts-ignore`, 200 LOC soft cap per file, kebab-case files, given/when/then tests.
- Do NOT auto-migrate users' existing explicit `"enabled": false` configs to true (honor their hard-off); only the auto-provision stub and the schema default change.

## Verification strategy
> Zero human intervention - all verification is agent-executed.
- Test decision: **TDD** (Bun `bun:test`, co-located `*.test.ts`, given/when/then). Write the failing test first for each code todo, then implement to green.
- Gates per todo: `bun test` (relevant suites) green; `bun run typecheck` clean; `bun run build:schema` regenerates `assets/oh-my-opencode.schema.json` for schema todos; `prompt-async-route-audit.test.ts` + `mock-module-lifecycle-audit.test.ts` stay green.
- LIVE QA (mandatory, blocks done): the `opencode-qa` skill drives the real harness in an isolated XDG sandbox (`source script/agent/qa-sandbox.sh`) to prove sidebar render + click-toggle + outbound sent→read transition + a no-restart permission change. Evidence (commands, observed output, isolation proof via session-count before/after) under `.omo/evidence/<YYYYMMDD>-mailbox-tui-hot-reload/`.
- Evidence: `.omo/evidence/task-<N>-mailbox-tui-sidebar-and-hot-reload.<ext>` per todo; live-QA bundle under `.omo/evidence/<date>-mailbox-tui-hot-reload/`.

## Execution strategy
### Parallel execution waves
> Target 5-8 todos per wave. Fewer than 3 (except the final) means you under-split.
- **Wave 1 - Foundation (T1-T4, parallel):** schema default flip, outbox-log normalization, omo tui-preferences helper, auto-provision stub. Independent files; no cross-dependencies.
- **Wave 2 - Logic (T5-T7, parallel after W1):** C1 sidebar-state reader (needs T2's normalized outbox), send-tool lazy reload (needs T1), idle-drain lazy reload + permissionless early-out (needs T1).
- **Wave 3 - Render + toggle (T8-T10, mostly serial after W2):** readView/computeView population + viewKey extension (needs T5), buildViewNodes mailbox section (needs T8 fields), tui.ts collapse closure wiring (needs T9 render + T3 helper).
- **Final wave (F1-F4):** after all todos, in parallel, including the live opencode-qa run.

| Todo | Depends on | Blocks | Can parallelize with |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T1 schema default | - | T5,T6,T7 | T2,T3,T4 |
| T2 outbox normalize | - | T5 | T1,T3,T4 |

| T8 readView+viewKey | T5 | T9 | - |

- [ ] F3. Real manual QA (opencode-qa, LIVE - blocks done) - in an isolated XDG sandbox (`source script/agent/qa-sandbox.sh`), drive the real harness: (1) sidebar renders the Mailbox section with correct inbound/outbound counts; (2) click the header → collapses/expands (▶/▼); (3) send A→B, drain on B, observe A's outbound flip sent→read; (4) a quarantined note shows as failed; (5) edit senders/budget in a project config and observe the next send + next idle drain honor it with NO session restart; (6) prove isolation (session-count before/after unchanged on the real DB). Evidence bundle under `.omo/evidence/<date>-mailbox-tui-hot-reload/` with commands, observed output, isolation proof.
- [ ] F4. Scope fidelity - no `allow-interrupt`, no `fs.watch`, no unbounded outbox scan, no raw promptAsync, registry hot-path untouched, existing explicit `enabled:false` still hard-off, no auto-migration of user configs.

## Commit strategy
- One commit per todo (types/scopes as listed on each todo's Commit line); `feat(mailbox)`/`fix(mailbox)`/`feat(tui)`.
- Delivered via the `work-with-pr` skill: isolated git worktree → TDD per todo → scoped `opencode-qa` evidence under `.omo/evidence/` → reviewer-readable English PR → verification loop → merge COMMIT into `dev` (never squash/rebase-merge). PR targets `dev`, never `master`.
- Do NOT commit `.cortexkit/`. Do NOT modify `package.json` version. Run `bun run build:schema` and commit the regenerated `assets/oh-my-opencode.schema.json` with T1.

## Success criteria
- The omo TUI sidebar shows a Mailbox section (active + idle views) with accurate unresolved inbound + 3-state outbound (sent/read/failed) counts.
- The header ▶/▼ click-toggle expands/collapses the section and the choice persists across restarts via `~/.config/opencode/tui-preferences.jsonc` key `"oh-my-openagent"`, without clobbering sibling plugin keys.
- Editing a project's `cross_project_mailbox` (senders/budget/bounds) is honored by the next `project_message` send and the next idle drain with NO session restart; a permissionless project's idle drain is a cheap no-op.
- `cross_project_mailbox` defaults to a populated `enabled:true` block (omitted block → on-but-permissionless); explicit `enabled:false` remains an honored hard-off.
- TDD throughout; `bun test`, `typecheck`, `build`, `build:schema`, prompt-async-route-audit all green; LIVE opencode-qa evidence recorded under `.omo/evidence/<date>-mailbox-tui-hot-reload/`.

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin/tool-registry-mailbox-tools.ts' in /Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import path from "node:path"
     2	
     3	import type { ToolDefinition } from "@opencode-ai/plugin"
     4	import type { OhMyOpenCodeConfig } from "../config"
     5	import { createProjectMessageTool } from "../tools"
     6	import { projectIdForRoot } from "../features/cross-project-mailbox/envelope"
     7	import { createProjectRegistry } from "../features/cross-project-mailbox/registry"
     8	import type { PluginContext } from "./types"
     9	import type { ToolRegistryFactories } from "./tool-registry-factories"
    10	
    11	export function createMailboxToolsRecord(args: {
    12	  readonly pluginConfig: OhMyOpenCodeConfig
    13	  readonly ctx: PluginContext
    14	  readonly factories?: Pick<ToolRegistryFactories, "createProjectMessageTool">
    15	}): Record<string, ToolDefinition> {
    16	  const { pluginConfig, ctx, factories } = args
    17	  const config = pluginConfig.cross_project_mailbox
    18	  if (!config?.enabled) return {}
    19	
    20	  const factory = factories?.createProjectMessageTool ?? createProjectMessageTool
    21	  const repoRoot = ctx.directory
    22	  const registry = createProjectRegistry()
    23	
    24	  return {
    25	    project_message: factory({
    26	      config,
    27	      thisProjectId: projectIdForRoot(repoRoot),
    28	      thisRepoRoot: repoRoot,
    29	      thisProjectDisplayName: path.basename(repoRoot),
    30	      registry: { listProjects: () => registry.listProjects() },
    31	    }),
    32	  }
    33	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import type { HookName, OhMyOpenCodeConfig } from "../../config"
     2	import { autoProvisionMailboxConfig } from "../../features/cross-project-mailbox/auto-provision"
     3	import { createMailboxHooks, type MailboxHooks } from "../../features/cross-project-mailbox/hooks"
     4	import { safeCreateHook } from "../../shared/safe-create-hook"
     5	import type { PluginContext } from "../types"
     6	
     7	export type MailboxSessionHooks = {
     8	  mailboxIdleDrain: MailboxHooks["mailboxIdleDrain"]
     9	}
    10	
    11	export function createMailboxSessionHooks(args: {
    12	  ctx: PluginContext
    13	  pluginConfig: OhMyOpenCodeConfig
    14	  isHookEnabled: (hookName: HookName) => boolean
    15	  safeHookEnabled: boolean
    16	}): MailboxSessionHooks {
    17	  const { ctx, pluginConfig, isHookEnabled, safeHookEnabled } = args
    18	  const config = pluginConfig.cross_project_mailbox
    19	
    20	  if (!config) {
    21	    autoProvisionMailboxConfig(ctx.directory)
    22	  }
    23	
    24	  const mailboxIdleDrain =
    25	    isHookEnabled("cross-project-mailbox-idle-drain") && config?.enabled
    26	      ? safeCreateHook(
    27	          "cross-project-mailbox-idle-drain",
    28	          () => createMailboxHooks(ctx, config).mailboxIdleDrain,
    29	          { enabled: safeHookEnabled },
    30	        )
    31	      : null
    32	
    33	  return { mailboxIdleDrain }
    34	}

     9	const NOTE_SUFFIX = ".md"
    10	const RECENT_SENT_LIMIT = 3
    11	
    12	export interface MailboxSidebarState {
    13	  inboundUnread: number
    14	  inboundProcessed: number
    15	  recentSentCount: number
    16	  recentSent: OutboxEntry[]
    17	}
    18	
    19	export async function readMailboxSidebarState(
    20	  repoRoot: string,
    21	  config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig,
    22	): Promise<MailboxSidebarState | null> {
    23	  if (!config.enabled) return null
    24	
    25	  const senderDirs = await readSenderDirs(repoRoot)
    26	  let inboundUnread = 0
    27	  let inboundProcessed = 0
    28	  for (const sender of senderDirs) {
    29	    const senderPath = path.join(repoRoot, "coordination_notes", sender)
    30	    inboundUnread += await countNotes(senderPath)
    31	    inboundProcessed += await countNotes(path.join(senderPath, "processed"))
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  const { recentSent, recentSentCount } = await readRecentSent(repoRoot)
    35	
    36	  return { inboundUnread, inboundProcessed, recentSentCount, recentSent }
    37	}
    38	
    39	async function readSenderDirs(repoRoot: string): Promise<string[]> {
    40	  const notesRoot = path.join(repoRoot, "coordination_notes")
    41	  const entries = await readDirSafe(notesRoot)
    42	  return entries.filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory()).map((entry) => entry.name)
    43	}
    44	
    45	async function countNotes(dir: string): Promise<number> {
    46	  const entries = await readDirSafe(dir)
    47	  return entries.filter(isNoteFile).length
    48	}
    49	
    50	function isNoteFile(entry: Dirent): boolean {
    51	  return entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith(NOTE_SUFFIX) && !entry.name.startsWith(".")
    52	}
    53	
    54	async function readDirSafe(dir: string): Promise<Dirent[]> {
    55	  try {
    56	    return await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true })
    57	  } catch {
    58	    return []
    59	  }
    60	}
    61	
    62	async function readRecentSent(
    63	  repoRoot: string,
    64	): Promise<{ recentSent: OutboxEntry[]; recentSentCount: number }> {
    65	  let raw: string
    66	  try {
    67	    raw = await readFile(outboxLogPath(repoRoot), "utf8")
    68	  } catch {
    69	    return { recentSent: [], recentSentCount: 0 }
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  const entries = raw
    73	    .split("\n")
    74	    .map((line) => line.trim())
    75	    .filter((line) => line.length > 0)
    76	    .map(parseOutboxLine)
    77	    .filter((entry): entry is OutboxEntry => entry !== null)
    78	
    79	  const recentSent = entries.slice(-RECENT_SENT_LIMIT).reverse()
    80	  return { recentSent, recentSentCount: entries.length }
    81	}
    82	
    83	function parseOutboxLine(line: string): OutboxEntry | null {
    84	  try {
    85	    return JSON.parse(line) as OutboxEntry
    86	  } catch {
    87	    return null
    88	  }
    89	}

     1	import type { Dirent } from "node:fs"
     2	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto"
     3	import { mkdir, open, readdir, readFile, rename, rm, stat } from "node:fs/promises"
     4	import path from "node:path"
     5	
     6	import { parseEnvelope, serializeEnvelope } from "../envelope/schema"
     7	import type { MailboxMessage } from "../envelope/schema"
     8	import { assertPathWithinRoot, safeMessageIdFilename } from "../envelope/path-guard"
     9	import { PendingDeliveryStore } from "./pending-delivery-store"
    10	import type { MailboxDir, PendingEntry, QuarantineReason, UnreadMessage } from "./types"
    11	
    12	const RESERVED_PREFIX = ".delivering-"
    13	const NOTE_SUFFIX = ".md"
    14	
    15	function isMissingPathError(error: unknown): boolean {
    16	  return error instanceof Error && "code" in error && (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT"
    17	}
    18	
    19	function isUnreadNoteFile(entry: Dirent): boolean {
    20	  return entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith(NOTE_SUFFIX) && !entry.name.startsWith(".")
    21	}
    22	
    23	export class MailboxStore {
    24	  private readonly pendingStore: PendingDeliveryStore
    25	
    26	  constructor(
    27	    private readonly targetRepoRoot: string,
    28	    private readonly fromProjectId: string,
    29	    private readonly config: { reservation_ttl_ms: number },
    30	  ) {
    31	    this.pendingStore = new PendingDeliveryStore(this.targetRepoRoot)
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  async markDispatched(entry: Omit<PendingEntry, "state">): Promise<void> {
    35	    await this.pendingStore.addDispatchSent(entry)
    36	  }
    37	
    38	  private dirs(): MailboxDir {
    39	    const inbox = path.join(this.targetRepoRoot, "coordination_notes", this.fromProjectId)
    40	    return { inbox, processed: path.join(inbox, "processed"), rejected: path.join(inbox, "rejected") }
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  private guard(candidate: string): void {
    44	    assertPathWithinRoot(this.targetRepoRoot, candidate)
    45	  }
    46	
    47	  async writeNote(envelope: MailboxMessage, body: string): Promise<void> {
    48	    const { inbox } = this.dirs()
    49	    await mkdir(inbox, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 })
    50	    const fileName = safeMessageIdFilename(envelope.messageId)
    51	    const targetPath = path.join(inbox, fileName)
    52	    this.guard(targetPath)
    53	    const tmpPath = path.join(inbox, `.tmp-note-${randomUUID()}.md`)
    54	    this.guard(tmpPath)
    55	    const serialized = serializeEnvelope(envelope, body)
    56	    try {
    57	      const fileHandle = await open(tmpPath, "wx")
    58	      try {
    59	        await fileHandle.writeFile(serialized)
    60	      } finally {
    61	        await fileHandle.close()
    62	      }
    63	      await rename(tmpPath, targetPath)
    64	    } catch (error) {
    65	      await rm(tmpPath, { force: true })
    66	      throw error
    67	    }
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  async parseNote(filePath: string): Promise<{ envelope: MailboxMessage; body: string }> {
    71	    this.guard(filePath)
    72	    const fileContent = await readFile(filePath, "utf8")
    73	    return parseEnvelope(fileContent)
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  async listUnread(): Promise<UnreadMessage[]> {
    77	    const { inbox } = this.dirs()
    78	    let entries: Dirent[]
    79	    try {
    80	      entries = await readdir(inbox, { withFileTypes: true })
    81	    } catch (error) {
    82	      if (isMissingPathError(error)) return []
    83	      throw error
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    const notes: UnreadMessage[] = []
    87	    for (const entry of entries) {
    88	      if (!isUnreadNoteFile(entry)) continue
    89	      const filePath = path.join(inbox, entry.name)
    90	      try {
    91	        const parsed = await this.parseNote(filePath)
    92	        notes.push({ messageId: parsed.envelope.messageId, filePath, envelope: parsed.envelope, body: parsed.body })
    93	      } catch {
    94	        continue
    95	      }
    96	    }
    97	    return notes.sort((left, right) => left.envelope.timestamp - right.envelope.timestamp)
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  async reserve(messageId: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
   101	    const { inbox } = this.dirs()
   102	    const fileName = safeMessageIdFilename(messageId)
   103	    const inboxPath = path.join(inbox, fileName)
   104	    const reservedPath = path.join(inbox, `${RESERVED_PREFIX}${messageId}${NOTE_SUFFIX}`)
   105	    this.guard(inboxPath)
   106	    this.guard(reservedPath)
   107	    try {
   108	      await rename(inboxPath, reservedPath)
   109	      return reservedPath
   110	    } catch (error) {
   111	      if (isMissingPathError(error)) return undefined
   112	      throw error
   113	    }
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  async ack(messageId: string): Promise<void> {
   117	    const { inbox, processed } = this.dirs()
   118	    await mkdir(processed, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 })
   119	    const fileName = safeMessageIdFilename(messageId)
   120	    const targetPath = path.join(processed, fileName)
   121	    this.guard(targetPath)
   122	    const sourcePaths = [
   123	      path.join(inbox, `${RESERVED_PREFIX}${messageId}${NOTE_SUFFIX}`),
   124	      path.join(inbox, fileName),
   125	    ]
   126	    for (const sourcePath of sourcePaths) {
   127	      this.guard(sourcePath)
   128	      try {
   129	        await rename(sourcePath, targetPath)
   130	        return
   131	      } catch (error) {
   132	        if (isMissingPathError(error)) continue
   133	        throw error
   134	      }
   135	    }
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  async quarantine(messageId: string, reason: QuarantineReason, detail = ""): Promise<void> {
   139	    const { inbox, rejected } = this.dirs()
   140	    await mkdir(rejected, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 })
   141	    const fileName = safeMessageIdFilename(messageId)
   142	    const targetPath = path.join(rejected, fileName)
   143	    this.guard(targetPath)
   144	    const sourcePaths = [
   145	      path.join(inbox, fileName),
   146	      path.join(inbox, `${RESERVED_PREFIX}${messageId}${NOTE_SUFFIX}`),
   147	    ]
   148	    for (const sourcePath of sourcePaths) {
   149	      this.guard(sourcePath)
   150	      try {
   151	        await rename(sourcePath, targetPath)
   152	        break
   153	      } catch (error) {
   154	        if (isMissingPathError(error)) continue
   155	        throw error
   156	      }
   157	    }
   158	    const reasonPath = path.join(rejected, `${messageId}.reason.json`)
   159	    this.guard(reasonPath)
   160	    await Bun.write(reasonPath, `${JSON.stringify({ reason, detail, at: new Date().toISOString() }, null, 2)}\n`)
   161	  }
   162	
   163	  async drainUnread(maxNotes: number): Promise<UnreadMessage[]> {
   164	    const unread = await this.listUnread()
   165	    const supersededIds = new Set(
   166	      unread
   167	        .map((message) => message.envelope.supersedes)
   168	        .filter((value): value is string => value !== null),
   169	    )
   170	    const latest = unread.filter((message) => !supersededIds.has(message.messageId))
   171	    latest.sort(
   172	      (left, right) =>
   173	        right.envelope.priority - left.envelope.priority ||
   174	        left.envelope.timestamp - right.envelope.timestamp,
   175	    )
   176	    return latest.slice(0, maxNotes)
   177	  }
   178	
   179	  async reclaimStale(sessionMessageIds: Set<string>): Promise<void> {
   180	    const { inbox } = this.dirs()
   181	    const cutoff = Date.now() - this.config.reservation_ttl_ms
   182	    let entries: Dirent[]
   183	    try {
   184	      entries = await readdir(inbox, { withFileTypes: true })
   185	    } catch (error) {
   186	      if (isMissingPathError(error)) return
   187	      throw error
   188	    }
   189	
   190	    for (const entry of entries) {
   191	      if (!entry.isFile()) continue
   192	      if (!entry.name.startsWith(RESERVED_PREFIX) || !entry.name.endsWith(NOTE_SUFFIX)) continue
   193	      const filePath = path.join(inbox, entry.name)
   194	      const fileStat = await stat(filePath)
   195	      if (fileStat.mtimeMs > cutoff) continue
   196	      const messageId = entry.name.slice(RESERVED_PREFIX.length, -NOTE_SUFFIX.length)
   197	      await this.reclaimOne(messageId, this.pendingStore, sessionMessageIds)
   198	    }
   199	  }
   200	
   201	  private async reclaimOne(
   202	    messageId: string,
   203	    pending: PendingDeliveryStore,
   204	    sessionMessageIds: Set<string>,
   205	  ): Promise<void> {
   206	    const entry = await pending.getEntry(messageId)
   207	    if (entry?.state === "history_confirmed") {
   208	      await this.ack(messageId)
   209	      return
   210	    }
   211	    if (entry?.state === "dispatch_sent" && sessionMessageIds.has(messageId)) {
   212	      await pending.markHistoryConfirmed(messageId)
   213	      await this.ack(messageId)
   214	      return
   215	    }
   216	    await this.returnToUnread(messageId)
   217	    if (entry !== undefined) {
   218	      await pending.removeEntry(messageId)
   219	    }
   220	  }
   221	
   222	  private async returnToUnread(messageId: string): Promise<void> {
   223	    const { inbox } = this.dirs()
   224	    const reservedPath = path.join(inbox, `${RESERVED_PREFIX}${messageId}${NOTE_SUFFIX}`)
   225	    const inboxPath = path.join(inbox, safeMessageIdFilename(messageId))
   226	    this.guard(reservedPath)
   227	    this.guard(inboxPath)
   228	    try {
   229	      await rename(reservedPath, inboxPath)
   230	    } catch (error) {
   231	      if (isMissingPathError(error)) return
   232	      throw error
   233	    }
   234	  }
   235	}

     1	import { appendFile, mkdir } from "node:fs/promises"
     2	import path from "node:path"
     3	
     4	import type { IntentEnum } from "../validation/types"
     5	
     6	const BODY_PREVIEW_MAX = 100
     7	
     8	export interface OutboxEntry {
     9	  sentAt: number
    10	  toProjectId: string
    11	  messageId: string
    12	  intent: IntentEnum
    13	  correlationId: string
    14	  body: string
    15	}
    16	
    17	export function outboxLogPath(repoRoot: string): string {
    18	  return path.join(repoRoot, ".omo", "mailbox-outbox.jsonl")
    19	}
    20	
    21	export async function appendOutboxLog(repoRoot: string, entry: OutboxEntry): Promise<void> {
    22	  const logPath = outboxLogPath(repoRoot)
    23	  await mkdir(path.dirname(logPath), { recursive: true })
    24	  const record = {
    25	    sentAt: entry.sentAt,
    26	    toProjectId: entry.toProjectId,
    27	    messageId: entry.messageId,
    28	    intent: entry.intent,
    29	    correlationId: entry.correlationId,
    30	    body: entry.body.slice(0, BODY_PREVIEW_MAX),
    31	  }
    32	  await appendFile(logPath, `${JSON.stringify(record)}\n`, "utf8")
    33	}

The ack directory claim is confirmed so far: `ack()` moves notes to `processed/`, `quarantine()` moves them to `rejected/`, and `listUnread()` excludes `.delivering-*`. I’m now checking whether the plan’s lazy reload target is actually callable with the right semantics, which is the most likely place for hidden breakage.

    12	export const IntentEnumSchema = z.enum(MAILBOX_INTENTS)

    14	export function createProjectMessageInputSchema(maxBodyBytes: number) {
    15	  return z
    16	    .object({
    17	      targetProjectId: z.string(),
    18	      intent: IntentEnumSchema,
    19	      body: z.string().max(maxBodyBytes),

    31	  listProjects(): Promise<ProjectEntry[]>
    32	}

    34	export interface ProjectMessageToolDeps {
    35	  config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig
    36	  thisProjectId: string
    37	  thisRepoRoot: string
    38	  thisProjectDisplayName: string
    39	  registry: ProjectMessageRegistry
    40	  writeNote?: (targetRepoRoot: string, fromProjectId: string, envelope: MailboxMessage, body: string) => Promise<void>
    41	  appendOutbox?: typeof appendOutboxLog
    42	}
    43	
    44	export type SendResult =
    45	  | { ok: true; envelope: MailboxMessage; messageId: string; correlationId: string }
    46	  | { error: "target-not-found" | "reply-parent-not-found" }
    47	  | { blocked: true; reason: PreflightReason }
    48	
    49	async function defaultWriteNote(
    50	  targetRepoRoot: string,
    51	  fromProjectId: string,
    52	  envelope: MailboxMessage,
    53	  body: string,
    54	  reservationTtlMs: number,
    55	): Promise<void> {
    56	  const store = new MailboxStore(targetRepoRoot, fromProjectId, { reservation_ttl_ms: reservationTtlMs })
    57	  await store.writeNote(envelope, body)
    58	}
    59	
    60	export async function runProjectMessageSend(
    61	  input: SendInput,
    62	  deps: ProjectMessageToolDeps,
    63	): Promise<SendResult> {
    64	  const projects = await deps.registry.listProjects()
    65	  const targetEntry =
    66	    projects.find((entry) => entry.projectId === input.targetProjectId) ??
    67	    projects.find((entry) => entry.displayName.toLowerCase() === input.targetProjectId.toLowerCase())
    68	  if (targetEntry === undefined) {
    69	    return { error: "target-not-found" }
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  const normalizedInput: SendInput = { ...input, targetProjectId: targetEntry.projectId }
    73	
    74	  const built = await buildSendEnvelope(
    75	    normalizedInput,
    76	    deps.thisProjectId,
    77	    deps.thisRepoRoot,
    78	    targetEntry.projectId,
    79	    targetEntry.displayName,
    80	    deps.thisProjectDisplayName,
    81	  )
    82	  if ("error" in built) {
    83	    return { error: built.error }
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  const preflight = await runSendPreflight(normalizedInput, built.envelope.hopCount, deps.config, targetEntry)
    87	  if (preflight.blocked) {
    88	    return { blocked: true, reason: preflight.reason }
    89	  }
    90	
    91	  if (deps.writeNote) {
    92	    await deps.writeNote(targetEntry.repoRoot, deps.thisProjectId, built.envelope, built.body)
    93	  } else {
    94	    await defaultWriteNote(
    95	      targetEntry.repoRoot,
    96	      deps.thisProjectId,
    97	      built.envelope,
    98	      built.body,
    99	      deps.config.bounds.reservation_ttl_ms,
   100	    )
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  const append = deps.appendOutbox ?? appendOutboxLog
   104	  await append(deps.thisRepoRoot, {
   105	    sentAt: built.envelope.timestamp,
   106	    toProjectId: input.targetProjectId,
   107	    messageId: built.envelope.messageId,
   108	    intent: built.envelope.intent,
   109	    correlationId: built.envelope.correlationId,
   110	    body: built.body,
   111	  })
   112	
   113	  return {
   114	    ok: true,
   115	    envelope: built.envelope,
   116	    messageId: built.envelope.messageId,
   117	    correlationId: built.envelope.correlationId,
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	export function createProjectMessageTool(deps: ProjectMessageToolDeps): ToolDefinition {
   122	  const inputSchema = createProjectMessageInputSchema(deps.config.bounds.max_body_bytes)
   123	  return tool({
   124	    description: "Send a note to another registered project's agent session",
   125	    args: {
   126	      targetProjectId: tool.schema.string().describe("Registered projectId or display name of the destination project"),
   127	      intent: tool.schema.enum(MAILBOX_INTENTS).describe("Intent tier of this note"),
   128	      body: tool.schema.string().describe("Note body"),
   129	      priority: tool.schema.number().optional().default(0).describe("Optional priority; higher drains first"),
   130	      threadId: tool.schema.string().optional().describe("Optional correlation UUID for a fresh thread (ignored on replies)"),
   131	      supersedes: tool.schema.string().optional().describe("Optional messageId this note supersedes"),
   132	      inReplyToMessageId: tool.schema.string().optional().describe("Optional parent messageId when replying to a received note"),
   133	    },
   134	    execute: async (rawArgs) => {
   135	      const input = inputSchema.parse(rawArgs)
   136	      const result = await runProjectMessageSend(input, deps)
   137	      return JSON.stringify(result)
   138	    },
   139	  })
   140	}

     1	import crypto from "node:crypto"
     2	import fs from "node:fs"
     3	import path from "node:path"
     4	
     5	export function projectIdForRoot(repoRoot: string): string {
     6	  const canonical = fs.realpathSync(repoRoot)
     7	  const basename = path.basename(canonical)
     8	  const slug = basename.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "")
     9	  const hash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(canonical).digest("hex").slice(0, 8)
    10	  return `${slug}-${hash}`
    11	}

    57	  writeCalls: number
    58	  outboxCalls: number
    59	}
    60	
    61	function spyDeps(config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig): SpyHandle {
    62	  const handle: SpyHandle = {
    63	    writeCalls: 0,
    64	    outboxCalls: 0,
    65	    deps: {
    66	      config,
    67	      thisProjectId: THIS_PROJECT_ID,
    68	      thisRepoRoot,
    69	      thisProjectDisplayName: "Project C",
    70	      registry: { listProjects: async () => projects },
    71	      writeNote: async () => {
    72	        handle.writeCalls += 1
    73	      },
    74	      appendOutbox: async () => {
    75	        handle.outboxCalls += 1
    76	      },
    77	    },
    78	  }
    79	  return handle
    80	}
    81	
    82	function realDeps(config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig): ProjectMessageToolDeps {
    83	  return {
    84	    config,
    85	    thisProjectId: THIS_PROJECT_ID,
    86	    thisRepoRoot,
    87	    thisProjectDisplayName: "Project C",
    88	    registry: { listProjects: async () => projects },
    89	  }
    90	}

    92	async function writeParentNote(senderId: string, parent: MailboxMessage): Promise<void> {
    93	  const dir = path.join(thisRepoRoot, "coordination_notes", senderId, "processed")
    94	  await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true })
    95	  await writeFile(path.join(dir, `${parent.messageId}.md`), serializeEnvelope(parent, "parent body"))
    96	}

    98	function makeParent(overrides: Partial<MailboxMessage> = {}): MailboxMessage {
    99	  return {
   100	    version: 1,
   101	    messageId: randomUUID(),
   102	    timestamp: Date.now(),
   103	    correlationId: randomUUID(),
   104	    inReplyToMessageId: null,
   105	    fromProject: "Project A",
   106	    toProject: "Project B",
   107	    fromProjectId: "proj-a",
   108	    toProjectId: "proj-b",
   109	    intent: "quick",
   110	    priority: 0,
   111	    hopCount: 1,
   112	    hopPath: ["proj-a", "proj-b"],
   113	    supersedes: null,
   114	    ...overrides,
   115	  }
   116	}

     5	import { parseEnvelope } from "../envelope/schema"
     6	import type { MailboxMessage } from "../envelope/schema"
     7	import { safeMessageIdFilename } from "../envelope/path-guard"
     8	import type { IntentEnum } from "../validation/types"

    10	export interface SendInput {
    11	  targetProjectId: string
    12	  intent: IntentEnum
    13	  body: string
    14	  priority?: number
    15	  threadId?: string | null
    16	  supersedes?: string | null
    17	  inReplyToMessageId?: string | null
    18	}
    19	
    20	export interface BuiltEnvelope {
    21	  envelope: MailboxMessage
    22	  body: string
    23	}
    24	
    25	export interface ReplyParentNotFound {
    26	  error: "reply-parent-not-found"
    27	}
    28	
    29	function isMissingPath(error: unknown): boolean {
    30	  return error instanceof Error && "code" in error && (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT"
    31	}
    32	
    33	function hasReplyTarget(value: string | null | undefined): value is string {
    34	  return value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== ""
    35	}
    36	
    37	export async function findProcessedParent(
    38	  thisRepoRoot: string,
    39	  parentId: string,
    40	): Promise<MailboxMessage | undefined> {
    41	  const fileName = safeMessageIdFilename(parentId)
    42	  const baseDir = path.join(thisRepoRoot, "coordination_notes")
    43	  let senders
    44	  try {
    45	    senders = await readdir(baseDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    46	  } catch (error) {
    47	    if (isMissingPath(error)) return undefined
    48	    throw error
    49	  }
    50	  for (const sender of senders) {
    51	    if (!sender.isDirectory()) continue
    52	    const candidate = path.join(baseDir, sender.name, "processed", fileName)
    53	    try {
    54	      const content = await readFile(candidate, "utf8")
    55	      return parseEnvelope(content).envelope
    56	    } catch (error) {
    57	      if (isMissingPath(error)) continue
    58	      throw error
    59	    }
    60	  }
    61	  return undefined
    62	}
    63	
    64	export async function buildSendEnvelope(
    65	  input: SendInput,
    66	  thisProjectId: string,
    67	  thisRepoRoot: string,
    68	  targetProjectId: string,
    69	  targetProjectDisplayName: string,
    70	  thisProjectDisplayName: string,
    71	): Promise<BuiltEnvelope | ReplyParentNotFound> {
    72	  let hopCount: number
    73	  let hopPath: string[]
    74	  let correlationId: string
    75	  let inReplyToMessageId: string | null
    76	
    77	  if (hasReplyTarget(input.inReplyToMessageId)) {
    78	    const parent = await findProcessedParent(thisRepoRoot, input.inReplyToMessageId)
    79	    if (parent === undefined) {
    80	      return { error: "reply-parent-not-found" }
    81	    }
    82	    hopCount = parent.hopCount + 1
    83	    hopPath = [...parent.hopPath, thisProjectId]
    84	    correlationId = parent.correlationId
    85	    inReplyToMessageId = input.inReplyToMessageId
    86	  } else {
    87	    hopCount = 0
    88	    hopPath = [thisProjectId]
    89	    correlationId = hasReplyTarget(input.threadId) ? input.threadId : randomUUID()
    90	    inReplyToMessageId = null
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  const envelope: MailboxMessage = {
    94	    version: 1,
    95	    messageId: randomUUID(),
    96	    timestamp: Date.now(),
    97	    correlationId,
    98	    inReplyToMessageId,
    99	    fromProject: thisProjectDisplayName,
   100	    toProject: targetProjectDisplayName,
   101	    fromProjectId: thisProjectId,
   102	    toProjectId: targetProjectId,
   103	    intent: input.intent,
   104	    priority: input.priority ?? 0,
   105	    hopCount,
   106	    hopPath,
   107	    supersedes: input.supersedes ?? null,
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  return { envelope, body: input.body }
   111	}

    10	const LOCK_RETRY_MS = 50
    11	const LOCK_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000
    12	const LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS = 300_000
    13	
    14	function delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    15	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    16	    setTimeout(resolve, ms)
    17	  })

    20	function sortEntries(entries: readonly ProjectEntry[]): ProjectEntry[] {
    21	  return [...entries].sort(
    22	    (left, right) =>
    23	      right.lastSeen - left.lastSeen || left.displayName.localeCompare(right.displayName),
    24	  )
    25	}

    27	function isProjectEntry(value: unknown): value is ProjectEntry {
    28	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return false
    29	  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>
    30	  return (
    31	    typeof record["projectId"] === "string" &&
    32	    typeof record["repoRoot"] === "string" &&
    33	    typeof record["displayName"] === "string" &&
    34	    typeof record["lastSeen"] === "number"
    35	  )
    36	}

    38	function parseRegistryData(content: string): RegistryData {
    39	  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(content)
    40	  if (typeof parsed !== "object" || parsed === null) return { projects: [] }
    41	  const projects = (parsed as Record<string, unknown>)["projects"]
    42	  if (!Array.isArray(projects)) return { projects: [] }
    43	  return { projects: projects.filter(isProjectEntry) }
    44	}

    46	function buildLockOwnerContent(): string {
    47	  return `project-registry\n${process.pid}\n${Date.now()}\n`
    48	}
    49	
    50	function isPidAlive(pid: number): boolean {
    51	  try {
    52	    process.kill(pid, 0)
    53	    return true
    54	  } catch (error) {
    55	    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    56	    return false
    57	  }
    58	}
    59	
    60	async function isStaleLock(lockPath: string): Promise<boolean> {
    61	  try {
    62	    const content = await readFile(lockPath, "utf8")
    63	    const lines = content.split(/\r?\n/).filter((line) => line.length > 0)
    64	    if (lines.length !== 3) return false
    65	    const ownerPid = Number.parseInt(lines[1] ?? "", 10)
    66	    const acquiredAt = Number.parseInt(lines[2] ?? "", 10)
    67	    if (!Number.isInteger(ownerPid) || ownerPid <= 0) return false
    68	    if (!Number.isInteger(acquiredAt) || acquiredAt <= 0) return false
    69	    if (isPidAlive(ownerPid)) return false
    70	    return Date.now() - acquiredAt > LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS
    71	  } catch (error) {
    72	    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    73	    return false
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	async function releaseLock(lockPath: string): Promise<void> {
    78	  await unlink(lockPath).catch((error: unknown) => {
    79	    if (error instanceof Error) return undefined
    80	    return undefined
    81	  })
    82	}
    83	
    84	async function acquireLock(lockPath: string): Promise<void> {
    85	  const startedAt = Date.now()
    86	  for (;;) {
    87	    if (Date.now() - startedAt > LOCK_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
    88	      throw new Error(`Timed out acquiring lock: ${lockPath}`)
    89	    }
    90	    try {
    91	      const fileHandle = await open(lockPath, "wx")
    92	      try {
    93	        await fileHandle.writeFile(buildLockOwnerContent())
    94	      } finally {
    95	        await fileHandle.close()
    96	      }
    97	      return
    98	    } catch (error) {
    99	      const err = error as NodeJS.ErrnoException
   100	      if (err.code !== "EEXIST") throw error
   101	      if (await isStaleLock(lockPath)) {
   102	        await releaseLock(lockPath)
   103	        continue
   104	      }
   105	      await delay(LOCK_RETRY_MS)
   106	    }
   107	  }
   108	}
   109	
   110	export function defaultRegistryPath(): string {
   111	  return path.join(os.homedir(), ".omo", "project-registry.json")
   112	}
   113	
   114	export function createProjectRegistry(registryPath?: string): ProjectRegistry {
   115	  return new ProjectRegistry(registryPath ?? defaultRegistryPath())
   116	}
   117	
   118	export class ProjectRegistry {
   119	  constructor(private readonly registryPath: string) {}
   120	
   121	  async registerProject(repoRoot: string): Promise<void> {
   122	    const canonicalRoot = realpathSync(repoRoot)
   123	    const projectId = projectIdForRoot(canonicalRoot)
   124	    const entry: ProjectEntry = {
   125	      projectId,
   126	      repoRoot: canonicalRoot,
   127	      displayName: path.basename(canonicalRoot),
   128	      lastSeen: Date.now(),
   129	    }
   130	    await this.withRegistryLock(async () => {
   131	      const data = await this.readRegistry()
   132	      const merged = data.projects.filter((existing) => existing.projectId !== projectId)
   133	      merged.push(entry)
   134	      await this.atomicWrite({ projects: sortEntries(merged) })
   135	    })
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  async resolveTargetRepoRoot(projectId: string): Promise<string> {
   139	    const data = await this.readRegistry()
   140	    const match = data.projects.find((entry) => entry.projectId === projectId)
   141	    if (match === undefined) throw new ProjectNotFoundError(projectId)
   142	    return match.repoRoot
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  async listProjects(): Promise<ProjectEntry[]> {
   146	    const data = await this.readRegistry()
   147	    return sortEntries(data.projects)
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  async discoverFromOpenClaw(openclawRegistryPath: string): Promise<void> {
   151	    let content: string
   152	    try {
   153	      content = await readFile(openclawRegistryPath, "utf8")
   154	    } catch (error) {
   155	      if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
   156	      return
   157	    }
   158	    const candidatePaths: string[] = []
   159	    for (const line of content.split("\n")) {
   160	      const trimmed = line.trim()
   161	      if (trimmed.length === 0) continue
   162	      let parsed: unknown
   163	      try {
   164	        parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed)
   165	      } catch (error) {
   166	        if (error instanceof SyntaxError) continue
   167	        throw error
   168	      }
   169	      if (typeof parsed !== "object" || parsed === null) continue
   170	      const projectPath = (parsed as Record<string, unknown>)["projectPath"]
   171	      if (typeof projectPath === "string" && projectPath.length > 0) {
   172	        candidatePaths.push(projectPath)
   173	      }
   174	    }
   175	    for (const projectPath of candidatePaths) {
   176	      try {
   177	        await this.registerProject(projectPath)
   178	      } catch (error) {
   179	        if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
   180	      }
   181	    }
   182	  }
   183	
   184	  private async readRegistry(): Promise<RegistryData> {
   185	    try {
   186	      const content = await readFile(this.registryPath, "utf8")
   187	      return parseRegistryData(content)
   188	    } catch (error) {
   189	      const err = error as NodeJS.ErrnoException
   190	      if (err.code === "ENOENT") return { projects: [] }
   191	      if (error instanceof SyntaxError) return { projects: [] }
   192	      throw error
   193	    }
   194	  }
   195	
   196	  private async atomicWrite(data: RegistryData): Promise<void> {
   197	    await mkdir(path.dirname(this.registryPath), { recursive: true })
   198	    const tmpPath = `${this.registryPath}.tmp-registry-${randomUUID()}.json`
   199	    const content = `${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}\n`
   200	    try {
   201	      const fileHandle = await open(tmpPath, "wx")
   202	      try {
   203	        await fileHandle.writeFile(content)
   204	      } finally {
   205	        await fileHandle.close()
   206	      }
   207	      await rename(tmpPath, this.registryPath)
   208	    } catch (error) {
   209	      await rm(tmpPath, { force: true })
   210	      throw error
   211	    }
   212	  }
   213	
   214	  private async withRegistryLock<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
   215	    await mkdir(path.dirname(this.registryPath), { recursive: true })
   216	    const lockPath = `${this.registryPath}.lock`
   217	    await acquireLock(lockPath)
   218	    try {
   219	      return await fn()
   220	    } finally {
   221	      await releaseLock(lockPath)
   222	    }
   223	  }
   224	}

    68	function buildDispatchArgs(
    69	  deps: IdleDrainHookDeps,
    70	  sessionId: string,
    71	  triageText: string,
    72	): AsyncDispatchArgs {
    73	  return {
    74	    mode: "async",
    75	    client: deps.client,
    76	    sessionID: sessionId,
    77	    source: IDLE_DRAIN_SOURCE,
    78	    input: {
    79	      path: { id: sessionId },
    80	      body: { parts: [{ type: "text", text: triageText }] },
    81	      query: { directory: deps.directory },
    82	    },
    83	  }
    84	}
    85	
    86	async function processNote(
    87	  deps: IdleDrainHookDeps,
    88	  store: MailboxStorePort,
    89	  digestStore: DigestStorePort,
    90	  rateLimiter: RateLimiterPort,
    91	  sessionId: string,
    92	  note: UnreadMessage,
    93	): Promise<boolean> {
    94	  const envelope = note.envelope
    95	
    96	  const duplicate = await digestStore.checkAndRecord({
    97	    fromProjectId: envelope.fromProjectId,
    98	    toProjectId: envelope.toProjectId,
    99	    correlationId: envelope.correlationId,
   100	    body: note.body,
   101	  })
   102	  if (duplicate.isDuplicate) {
   103	    const dupResult = deps.validateInbound(envelope, deps.config, { duplicateLoop: true })
   104	    await store.quarantine(note.messageId, dupResult.reason ?? "duplicate-loop", dupResult.detail ?? "")
   105	    return false
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  const rateLimit = await rateLimiter.checkRateLimit(envelope.fromProjectId, envelope.toProjectId)
   109	  if (rateLimit.limited) {
   110	    return false
   111	  }
   112	
   113	  const validation = deps.validateInbound(envelope, deps.config)
   114	  if (!validation.valid) {
   115	    await store.quarantine(note.messageId, validation.reason ?? "malformed", validation.detail ?? "")
   116	    return false
   117	  }
   118	
   119	  const reservedPath = await store.reserve(note.messageId)
   120	  if (reservedPath === undefined) {
   121	    return false
   122	  }
   123	
   124	  const triageText = deps.buildTriagePrompt(
   125	    { ...envelope, body: note.body },
   126	    { projectDisplayName: deps.projectDisplayName },
   127	  )
   128	  const dispatchResult = await deps.dispatchInternalPrompt({
   129	    ...buildDispatchArgs(deps, sessionId, triageText),
   130	    queueBehavior: "defer",
   131	  })
   132	  if (!isInternalPromptDispatchAccepted(dispatchResult)) {
   133	    return false
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  await store.markDispatched({
   137	    messageId: note.messageId,
   138	    sessionId,
   139	    reservedPath,
   140	    dispatchedAt: Date.now(),
   141	  })
   142	  return true
   143	}
   144	
   145	export function createIdleDrainHook(deps: IdleDrainHookDeps): {
   146	  "session.idle": (input: { sessionId: string }) => Promise<void>
   147	} {
   148	  return {
   149	    "session.idle": async ({ sessionId }: { sessionId: string }): Promise<void> => {
   150	      if (!sessionId) return
   151	
   152	      const primary = deps.resolveActivePrimaryAgent(sessionId)
   153	      if (primary === undefined || !isEligiblePrimary(deps.config, primary)) {
   154	        return
   155	      }
   156	
   157	      const maxNotes = deps.config.bounds.max_notes_per_drain
   158	      const projects = deps.getRegisteredProjects()
   159	      const sessionMessageIds = new Set(await deps.getSessionMessages(sessionId))
   160	      const digestStore = deps.makeDigestStore(deps.repoRoot)
   161	      const rateLimiter = deps.makeRateLimiter(deps.repoRoot)
   162	
   163	      let injected = 0
   164	      for (const sender of projects) {
   165	        if (injected >= maxNotes) break
   166	        const store = deps.makeMailboxStore(deps.repoRoot, sender.projectId)
   167	        await store.reclaimStale(sessionMessageIds)
   168	        const candidates = await store.drainUnread(maxNotes)
   169	        for (const note of candidates) {
   170	          if (injected >= maxNotes) break
   171	          const dispatched = await processNote(deps, store, digestStore, rateLimiter, sessionId, note)
   172	          if (dispatched) injected += 1
   173	        }
   174	      }
   175	    },
   176	  }
   177	}

     1	import type { CrossProjectMailboxConfig } from "../config"
     2	import type { ProjectEntry } from "../registry/types"

     7	export type ProjectRegistryEntry = ProjectEntry

     9	export type PreflightReason = "unauthorized" | "over-budget" | "hop-exceeded" | "target-not-found"
    10	
    11	export type PreflightResult = { blocked: false } | { blocked: true; reason: PreflightReason }
    12	
    13	const LOWEST_CEILING: IntentEnum = "question"
    14	
    15	interface SenderDecision {
    16	  allowed: boolean
    17	  ceiling: IntentEnum
    18	}
    19	
    20	function resolveSenderDecision(input: SendInput, config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig): SenderDecision {
    21	  const sender = config.senders[input.targetProjectId]
    22	  if (sender !== undefined) {
    23	    return { allowed: sender.access === "allow", ceiling: sender.intent_budget }
    24	  }
    25	  const allowed = config.default_sender_access === "allow-all"
    26	  return { allowed, ceiling: LOWEST_CEILING }
    27	}
    28	
    29	export async function runSendPreflight(
    30	  input: SendInput,
    31	  proposedHopCount: number,
    32	  config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig,
    33	  targetEntry: ProjectRegistryEntry | undefined,
    34	): Promise<PreflightResult> {
    35	  if (targetEntry === undefined) {
    36	    return { blocked: true, reason: "target-not-found" }
    37	  }
    38	
    39	  const decision = resolveSenderDecision(input, config)
    40	  if (!decision.allowed) {
    41	    return { blocked: true, reason: "unauthorized" }
    42	  }
    43	
    44	  if (!withinBudget(input.intent, decision.ceiling)) {
    45	    return { blocked: true, reason: "over-budget" }
    46	  }
    47	
    48	  if (proposedHopCount >= config.bounds.max_hops) {
    49	    return { blocked: true, reason: "hop-exceeded" }
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  return { blocked: false }
    53	}

     1	import type { CrossProjectMailboxConfig } from "../config"
     2	import type { MailboxMessage } from "../envelope/schema"
     3	import type { IntentEnum, ValidateOptions, ValidationResult } from "./types"
     4	
     5	export const INTENT_LADDER: IntentEnum[] = ["question", "quick", "impl", "review", "work-loop", "plan"]
     6	
     7	const LOWEST_CEILING: IntentEnum = "question"
     8	
     9	export function withinBudget(noteIntent: IntentEnum, budgetCeiling: IntentEnum): boolean {
    10	  return INTENT_LADDER.indexOf(noteIntent) <= INTENT_LADDER.indexOf(budgetCeiling)
    11	}
    12	
    13	const REQUIRED_FIELDS = [
    14	  "messageId",
    15	  "fromProjectId",
    16	  "intent",
    17	  "hopCount",
    18	  "hopPath",
    19	  "correlationId",
    20	  "timestamp",
    21	] as const
    22	
    23	function findMissingField(note: MailboxMessage): string | undefined {
    24	  for (const field of REQUIRED_FIELDS) {
    25	    const value = note[field]
    26	    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
    27	      return field
    28	    }
    29	  }
    30	  return undefined
    31	}
    32	
    33	interface SenderDecision {
    34	  allowed: boolean
    35	  ceiling: IntentEnum
    36	}
    37	
    38	function resolveSenderDecision(note: MailboxMessage, config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig): SenderDecision {
    39	  const sender = config.senders[note.fromProjectId]
    40	  if (sender !== undefined) {
    41	    return { allowed: sender.access === "allow", ceiling: sender.intent_budget }
    42	  }
    43	  const allowed = config.default_sender_access === "allow-all"
    44	  return { allowed, ceiling: LOWEST_CEILING }
    45	}
    46	
    47	export function validateInbound(
    48	  note: MailboxMessage,
    49	  config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig,
    50	  opts: ValidateOptions = {},
    51	): ValidationResult {
    52	  const missingField = findMissingField(note)
    53	  if (missingField !== undefined) {
    54	    return { valid: false, reason: "malformed", detail: `missing required field: ${missingField}` }
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  if (note.hopCount >= config.bounds.max_hops) {
    58	    return {
    59	      valid: false,
    60	      reason: "hop-exceeded",
    61	      detail: `hopCount ${note.hopCount} >= max_hops ${config.bounds.max_hops}`,
    62	    }
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  if (opts.duplicateLoop === true) {
    66	    return { valid: false, reason: "duplicate-loop", detail: "note flagged as duplicate loop" }
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  const decision = resolveSenderDecision(note, config)
    70	  if (!decision.allowed) {
    71	    return { valid: false, reason: "unauthorized", detail: `sender ${note.fromProjectId} is not authorized` }
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  if (!withinBudget(note.intent, decision.ceiling)) {
    75	    return {
    76	      valid: false,
    77	      reason: "over-budget",
    78	      detail: `intent ${note.intent} exceeds ceiling ${decision.ceiling}`,
    79	    }
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  return { valid: true }
    83	}

    21	function loadSessionMessageIds(
    22	  ctx: PluginContext,
    23	  sessionId: string,
    24	): Promise<string[]> {
    25	  const messagesApi = ctx.client?.session?.messages
    26	  if (typeof messagesApi !== "function") return Promise.resolve([])
    27	  return Promise.resolve(
    28	    messagesApi({ path: { id: sessionId }, query: { directory: ctx.directory } }),
    29	  )
    30	    .then((result) => {
    31	      const data = (result as { data?: unknown })?.data ?? result
    32	      if (!Array.isArray(data)) return []
    33	      const ids: string[] = []
    34	      for (const entry of data) {
    35	        const id = (entry as { info?: { id?: unknown }; id?: unknown })?.info?.id
    36	          ?? (entry as { id?: unknown })?.id
    37	        if (typeof id === "string") ids.push(id)
    38	      }
    39	      return ids
    40	    })
    41	    .catch(() => [])
    42	}
    43	
    44	function buildIdleDrainDeps(
    45	  ctx: PluginContext,
    46	  config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig,
    47	): IdleDrainHookDeps {
    48	  const repoRoot = ctx.directory
    49	  const registry = createProjectRegistry()
    50	  let projectsSnapshot: ProjectEntry[] = []
    51	  const refreshSnapshot = (): void => {
    52	    registry
    53	      .listProjects()
    54	      .then((projects) => {
    55	        projectsSnapshot = projects.filter((entry) => entry.repoRoot !== repoRoot)
    56	      })
    57	      .catch((error) => {
    58	        log("mailbox refresh snapshot failed", { error })
    59	      })
    60	  }
    61	  refreshSnapshot()
    62	
    63	  return {
    64	    config,
    65	    repoRoot,
    66	    directory: ctx.directory,
    67	    projectDisplayName: path.basename(repoRoot),
    68	    client: ctx.client as IdleDrainHookDeps["client"],
    69	    resolveActivePrimaryAgent,
    70	    getRegisteredProjects: () => {
    71	      refreshSnapshot()
    72	      return projectsSnapshot
    73	    },
    74	    makeMailboxStore: (targetRoot, fromProjectId) =>
    75	      new MailboxStore(targetRoot, fromProjectId, {
    76	        reservation_ttl_ms: config.bounds.reservation_ttl_ms,
    77	      }),
    78	    makePendingStore: (targetRoot) => new PendingDeliveryStore(targetRoot),
    79	    makeDigestStore: (root) =>
    80	      new BodyDigestStore(root, config.bounds.body_digest_ttl_min * 60_000),
    81	    makeRateLimiter: (root) =>
    82	      new SamePairRateLimiter(root, config.bounds.same_pair_rate_limit_per_min),
    83	    validateInbound,
    84	    buildTriagePrompt,
    85	    dispatchInternalPrompt,
    86	    getSessionMessages: (sessionId) => loadSessionMessageIds(ctx, sessionId),
    87	  }
    88	}

    90	export function createMailboxHooks(
    91	  ctx: PluginContext,
    92	  config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig | undefined,
    93	): MailboxHooks {
    94	  if (!config?.enabled) return { mailboxIdleDrain: null }
    95	  return { mailboxIdleDrain: createIdleDrainHook(buildIdleDrainDeps(ctx, config)) }
    96	}

    38	interface TwoRepoEnv {
    39	  senderRoot: string
    40	  receiverRoot: string
    41	  registryHome: string
    42	  senderProjectId: string
    43	  receiverProjectId: string
    44	  dispatched: string[]
    45	  buildConfig: (overrides?: Partial<Record<string, unknown>>) => CrossProjectMailboxConfig
    46	  makeEnvelope: (overrides?: Partial<MailboxMessage>) => MailboxMessage
    47	  writeRawNote: (envelope: MailboxMessage, body: string) => Promise<void>
    48	  sendMessage: (input: { intent: MailboxMessage["intent"]; body: string }) => Promise<MailboxMessage>
    49	  runIdle: (config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig, opts?: RunOpts) => Promise<void>
    50	  inboxNotePath: (messageId: string) => string
    51	  processedNotePath: (messageId: string) => string
    52	  rejectedNotePath: (messageId: string) => string
    53	}

    55	async function createTempDir(prefix: string): Promise<string> {
    56	  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), prefix))
    57	  tempRoots.push(dir)
    58	  return dir
    59	}
    60	
    61	function extractDispatchedText(input: unknown): string | undefined {
    62	  const typed = input as { body?: { parts?: ReadonlyArray<{ text?: unknown }> } }
    63	  const text = typed.body?.parts?.[0]?.text
    64	  return typeof text === "string" ? text : undefined
    65	}
    66	
    67	async function createEnv(): Promise<TwoRepoEnv> {
    68	  const senderRoot = await createTempDir("omo-mailbox-sender-")
    69	  const receiverRoot = await createTempDir("omo-mailbox-receiver-")
    70	  const registryHome = await createTempDir("omo-mailbox-registry-")
    71	
    72	  const senderProjectId = projectIdForRoot(senderRoot)
    73	  const receiverProjectId = projectIdForRoot(receiverRoot)
    74	
    75	  const registry = createProjectRegistry(path.join(registryHome, ".omo", "project-registry.json"))
    76	  await registry.registerProject(senderRoot)
    77	  await registry.registerProject(receiverRoot)
    78	
    79	  const dispatched: string[] = []
    80	
    81	  const buildConfig = (overrides: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}): CrossProjectMailboxConfig =>
    82	    CrossProjectMailboxConfigSchema.parse({
    83	      enabled: true,
    84	      intake_eligible_agents: ["sisyphus"],
    85	      default_sender_access: "allow-all",
    86	      senders: { [senderProjectId]: { access: "allow", intent_budget: "impl" } },
    87	      ...overrides,
    88	    })
    89	
    90	  const makeEnvelope = (overrides: Partial<MailboxMessage> = {}): MailboxMessage => ({
    91	    version: 1,
    92	    messageId: randomUUID(),
    93	    timestamp: Date.now(),
    94	    correlationId: randomUUID(),
    95	    inReplyToMessageId: null,
    96	    fromProject: "sender",
    97	    toProject: "receiver",
    98	    fromProjectId: senderProjectId,
    99	    toProjectId: receiverProjectId,
   100	    intent: "impl",
   101	    priority: 0,
   102	    hopCount: 0,
   103	    hopPath: [senderProjectId],
   104	    supersedes: null,
   105	    ...overrides,
   106	  })
   107	
   108	  const writeRawNote = async (envelope: MailboxMessage, body: string): Promise<void> => {
   109	    const store = new MailboxStore(receiverRoot, senderProjectId, { reservation_ttl_ms: 120000 })
   110	    await store.writeNote(envelope, body)
   111	  }
   112	
   113	  const sendMessage = async (input: {
   114	    intent: MailboxMessage["intent"]
   115	    body: string
   116	  }): Promise<MailboxMessage> => {
   117	    const built = await buildSendEnvelope(
   118	      { targetProjectId: receiverProjectId, intent: input.intent, body: input.body },
   119	      senderProjectId,
   120	      senderRoot,
   121	      receiverProjectId,
   122	      "receiver",
   123	      "sender",
   124	    )
   125	    if ("error" in built) {
   126	      throw new Error(`buildSendEnvelope failed: ${built.error}`)
   127	    }
   128	    await writeRawNote(built.envelope, input.body)
   129	    await appendOutboxLog(senderRoot, {
   130	      sentAt: Date.now(),
   131	      toProjectId: receiverProjectId,
   132	      messageId: built.envelope.messageId,
   133	      intent: input.intent,
   134	      correlationId: built.envelope.correlationId,
   135	      body: input.body,
   136	    })
   137	    return built.envelope
   138	  }
   139	
   140	  const runIdle = async (config: CrossProjectMailboxConfig, opts: RunOpts = {}): Promise<void> => {
   141	    const projects = (await registry.listProjects()).filter(
   142	      (entry) => entry.projectId !== receiverProjectId,
   143	    )
   144	
   145	    const deps: IdleDrainHookDeps = {
   146	      config,
   147	      repoRoot: receiverRoot,
   148	      directory: receiverRoot,
   149	      projectDisplayName: "receiver",
   150	      client: { session: { promptAsync: async () => ({}) } },
   151	      resolveActivePrimaryAgent: () => ("primary" in opts ? opts.primary : "sisyphus"),
   152	      getRegisteredProjects: () => projects,
   153	      makeMailboxStore: (targetRoot, fromProjectId) =>
   154	        new MailboxStore(targetRoot, fromProjectId, {
   155	          reservation_ttl_ms: config.bounds.reservation_ttl_ms,
   156	        }),
   157	      makePendingStore: (targetRoot) => new PendingDeliveryStore(targetRoot),
   158	      makeDigestStore: (root) => new BodyDigestStore(root, config.bounds.body_digest_ttl_min * 60_000),
   159	      makeRateLimiter: (root) =>
   160	        new SamePairRateLimiter(root, config.bounds.same_pair_rate_limit_per_min),
   161	      validateInbound,
   162	      buildTriagePrompt,
   163	      dispatchInternalPrompt: async (args) => {
   164	        const text = extractDispatchedText(args.input)
   165	        if (text !== undefined) dispatched.push(text)
   166	        return { status: "dispatched", response: {} }
   167	      },
   168	      getSessionMessages: async () => opts.sessionMessages ?? [],
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    await createIdleDrainHook(deps)["session.idle"]({ sessionId: opts.sessionId ?? "ses_test" })
   172	  }
   173	
   174	  const inboxDir = path.join(receiverRoot, "coordination_notes", senderProjectId)
   175	
   176	  return {
   177	    senderRoot,
   178	    receiverRoot,
   179	    registryHome,
   180	    senderProjectId,
   181	    receiverProjectId,
   182	    dispatched,
   183	    buildConfig,
   184	    makeEnvelope,
   185	    writeRawNote,
   186	    sendMessage,
   187	    runIdle,
   188	    inboxNotePath: (messageId) => path.join(inboxDir, `${messageId}.md`),
   189	    processedNotePath: (messageId) => path.join(inboxDir, "processed", `${messageId}.md`),
   190	    rejectedNotePath: (messageId) => path.join(inboxDir, "rejected", `${messageId}.md`),
   191	  }
   192	}
