   194	describe("cross-project mailbox two-repo integration", () => {
   195	  describe("#given a sender and receiver repo wired through the registry", () => {
   196	    describe("#when the sender delivers a note and the receiver drains while idle", () => {
   197	      it("#then the note lands in the receiver mailbox directory", async () => {
   198	        // given
   199	        const env = await createEnv()
   200	
   201	        // when
   202	        const envelope = await env.sendMessage({ intent: "impl", body: "please implement the thing" })
   203	
   204	        // then
   205	        expect(existsSync(env.inboxNotePath(envelope.messageId))).toBe(true)
   206	      })
   207	
   208	      it("#then the idle drain dispatches the note body into the receiver session", async () => {
   209	        // given
   210	        const env = await createEnv()
   211	        await env.sendMessage({ intent: "impl", body: "ship the cross-project feature" })
   212	
   213	        // when
   214	        await env.runIdle(env.buildConfig())
   215	
   216	        // then
   217	        expect(env.dispatched).toHaveLength(1)
   218	        expect(env.dispatched[0]).toContain("ship the cross-project feature")
   219	      })
   220	
   221	      it("#then the sender outbox log records the sent message", async () => {
   222	        // given
   223	        const env = await createEnv()
   224	
   225	        // when
   226	        const envelope = await env.sendMessage({ intent: "impl", body: "log me to the outbox" })
   227	
   228	        // then
   229	        const logPath = outboxLogPath(env.senderRoot)
   230	        expect(existsSync(logPath)).toBe(true)
   231	        expect(readFileSync(logPath, "utf8")).toContain(envelope.messageId)
   232	      })
   233	
   234	      it("#then a second idle cycle acks the dispatched note into processed/", async () => {
   235	        // given
   236	        const env = await createEnv()
   237	        const config = env.buildConfig({
   238	          bounds: CrossProjectMailboxConfigSchema.parse({}).bounds,
   239	        })
   240	        const ackConfig = env.buildConfig({
   241	          bounds: { ...config.bounds, reservation_ttl_ms: 0 },
   242	        })
   243	        const envelope = await env.sendMessage({ intent: "impl", body: "drain then ack me" })
   244	
   245	        // when
   246	        await env.runIdle(ackConfig)
   247	        await env.runIdle(ackConfig, { sessionMessages: [envelope.messageId] })
   248	
   249	        // then
   250	        expect(env.dispatched).toHaveLength(1)
   251	        expect(existsSync(env.processedNotePath(envelope.messageId))).toBe(true)
   252	        expect(existsSync(env.inboxNotePath(envelope.messageId))).toBe(false)
   253	      })
   254	    })
   255	  })

   385	    })

    17	function makeConfig(overrides: Partial<CrossProjectMailboxConfig> = {}): CrossProjectMailboxConfig {
    18	  return {
    19	    enabled: true,
    20	    intake_eligible_agents: ["sisyphus"],
    21	    interrupt_policy: "idle-drain",
    22	    default_sender_access: "allow-all",
    23	    senders: {},
    24	    bounds: {
    25	      max_hops: 4,
    26	      max_notes_per_drain: 5,
    27	      same_pair_rate_limit_per_min: 6,
    28	      body_digest_ttl_min: 60,
    29	      max_body_bytes: 32768,
    30	      reservation_ttl_ms: 120000,
    31	    },
    32	    ...overrides,
    33	  }
    34	}
    35	
    36	function makeEnvelope(overrides: Partial<MailboxMessage> = {}): MailboxMessage {
    37	  return {
    38	    version: 1,
    39	    messageId: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
    40	    timestamp: 1000,
    41	    correlationId: "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222",
    42	    inReplyToMessageId: null,
    43	    fromProject: "alpha",
    44	    toProject: "beta",
    45	    fromProjectId: "alpha-id",
    46	    toProjectId: "beta-id",
    47	    intent: "impl",
    48	    priority: 0,
    49	    hopCount: 0,
    50	    hopPath: ["alpha-id"],
    51	    supersedes: null,
    52	    ...overrides,
    53	  }
    54	}
    55	
    56	function makeNote(overrides: Partial<MailboxMessage> = {}, body = "hello body"): UnreadMessage {
    57	  const envelope = makeEnvelope(overrides)
    58	  return { messageId: envelope.messageId, filePath: `/inbox/${envelope.messageId}.md`, envelope, body }
    59	}
    60	
    61	function makeProject(): ProjectEntry {
    62	  return { projectId: "alpha-id", repoRoot: "/repos/alpha", displayName: "alpha", lastSeen: 5 }
    63	}

    65	interface Spies {
    66	  reclaimStale: ReturnType<typeof jest.fn>
    67	  drainUnread: ReturnType<typeof jest.fn>
    68	  reserve: ReturnType<typeof jest.fn>

    84	function makeHarness(opts: {
    85	  config?: CrossProjectMailboxConfig
    86	  notes?: UnreadMessage[]
    87	  projects?: ProjectEntry[]
    88	  primary?: string | undefined
    89	  reserveResult?: string | undefined
    90	  duplicate?: boolean
    91	  rateLimited?: boolean
    92	  validation?: ValidationResult
    93	  sessionMessages?: string[]
    94	} = {}): { deps: IdleDrainHookDeps; spies: Spies } {
    95	  const config = opts.config ?? makeConfig()
    96	  const notes = opts.notes ?? [makeNote()]
    97	
    98	  const reclaimStale = jest.fn(async () => undefined)
    99	  const drainUnread = jest.fn(async () => notes)
   100	  const reserveResult = "reserveResult" in opts ? opts.reserveResult : "/inbox/.delivering-id.md"
   101	  const reserve = jest.fn(async () => reserveResult)
   102	  const quarantine = jest.fn(async () => undefined)
   103	  const markDispatched = jest.fn(async () => undefined)
   104	  const addDispatchSent = jest.fn(async () => undefined)
   105	  const checkAndRecord = jest.fn(async () => ({ isDuplicate: opts.duplicate ?? false }))
   106	  const checkRateLimit = jest.fn(async () => ({ limited: opts.rateLimited ?? false }))
   107	  const validateInbound = jest.fn(() => opts.validation ?? ({ valid: true } as ValidationResult))
   108	  const buildTriagePrompt = jest.fn(() => "TRIAGE_TEXT")
   109	  const dispatchInternalPrompt = jest.fn(async () => ({ status: "dispatched", response: {} }) as const)
   110	  const getSessionMessages = jest.fn(async () => opts.sessionMessages ?? [])
   111	  const resolveActivePrimaryAgent = jest.fn(() => ("primary" in opts ? opts.primary : "sisyphus"))
   112	  const getRegisteredProjects = jest.fn(() => opts.projects ?? [makeProject()])
   113	
   114	  const store: MailboxStorePort = { reclaimStale, drainUnread, reserve, quarantine, markDispatched }
   115	  const pending: PendingStorePort = { addDispatchSent }
   116	  const digest: DigestStorePort = { checkAndRecord }
   117	  const limiter: RateLimiterPort = { checkRateLimit }
   118	
   119	  const makeMailboxStore = jest.fn(() => store)
   120	  const makePendingStore = jest.fn(() => pending)
   121	
   122	  const deps: IdleDrainHookDeps = {
   123	    config,
   124	    repoRoot: "/repos/beta",
   125	    directory: "/repos/beta",
   126	    projectDisplayName: "beta",
   127	    client: { session: { promptAsync: jest.fn(async () => ({})) } },
   128	    resolveActivePrimaryAgent: resolveActivePrimaryAgent as never,
   129	    getRegisteredProjects: getRegisteredProjects as never,
   130	    makeMailboxStore: makeMailboxStore as never,
   131	    makePendingStore: makePendingStore as never,
   132	    makeDigestStore: jest.fn(() => digest) as never,
   133	    makeRateLimiter: jest.fn(() => limiter) as never,
   134	    validateInbound: validateInbound as never,
   135	    buildTriagePrompt: buildTriagePrompt as never,
   136	    dispatchInternalPrompt: dispatchInternalPrompt as never,
   137	    getSessionMessages: getSessionMessages as never,
   138	  }
   139	
   140	  return {
   141	    deps,
   142	    spies: {
   143	      reclaimStale,
   144	      drainUnread,
   145	      reserve,
   146	      quarantine,
   147	      markDispatched,
   148	      addDispatchSent,
   149	      checkAndRecord,
   150	      checkRateLimit,
   151	      validateInbound,
   152	      buildTriagePrompt,
   153	      dispatchInternalPrompt,
   154	      getSessionMessages,
   155	      makeMailboxStore,
   156	      makePendingStore,
   157	      resolveActivePrimaryAgent,
   158	      getRegisteredProjects,
   159	    },
   160	  }
   161	}

codex
The current drain hook closes over `deps.config` in more places than just validation: store constructors and digest/rate limiter factories also capture the original bounds. I’m checking the config loader API now to see if the proposed “existing merged loader” can be threaded without inventing a separate parse path.

    29	}

    31	type LoadedConfigLayer = ConfigLayer & {
    32	  readonly config: Partial<OhMyOpenCodeConfig> | null

    35	
    36	function resolveHomeDirectory(): string {
    37	  return process.env.HOME ?? process.env.USERPROFILE ?? homedir()
    38	}
    39	
    40	function discoverConfigInDirectory(configDir: string): string | null {
    41	  const detected = detectPluginConfigFile(configDir, {
    42	    basenames: [CONFIG_BASENAME],
    43	    legacyBasenames: [LEGACY_CONFIG_BASENAME],
    44	  })
    45	  return detected.format === "none" ? null : detected.path
    46	}
    47	
    48	function discoverUserLayers(): readonly ConfigLayer[] {
    49	  return getOpenCodeConfigDirs({ binary: "opencode" }).flatMap((configDir) => {
    50	    const configPath = discoverConfigInDirectory(configDir)
    51	    return configPath ? [{ path: configPath, configDir }] : []
    52	  })
    53	}
    54	
    55	function discoverProjectLayersNearestFirst(directory: string): readonly ConfigLayer[] {
    56	  const homeDirectory = resolveHomeDirectory()
    57	  const stopDirectory = containsPath(homeDirectory, directory) ? homeDirectory : directory
    58	  return findProjectOpencodePluginConfigFiles(directory, stopDirectory).map((configPath) => ({
    59	    path: configPath,
    60	    configDir: dirname(configPath),
    61	  }))
    62	}
    63	
    64	function shortPath(configPath: string): string {
    65	  const candidate = relative(process.cwd(), configPath)
    66	  return candidate.length > 0 ? candidate : configPath
    67	}
    68	
    69	function formatIssuePath(path: readonly PropertyKey[]): string {
    70	  const formatted = path.map((segment) => String(segment)).join(".")
    71	  return formatted.length > 0 ? formatted : "<root>"
    72	}
    73	
    74	function schemaMessages(configPath: string, rawConfig: Record<string, unknown>): readonly string[] {
    75	  const result = OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema.safeParse(rawConfig)
    76	  if (result.success) return []
    77	  return result.error.issues.map((issue) => `${shortPath(configPath)}: ${formatIssuePath(issue.path)}: ${issue.message}`)
    78	}
    79	
    80	function parseLayerConfig(configPath: string): LoadedConfigLayer {
    81	  try {
    82	    const content = readFileSync(configPath, "utf-8")
    83	    const rawConfig = parseJsonc<unknown>(content)
    84	    if (!isPlainRecord(rawConfig)) {
    85	      return {
    86	        path: configPath,
    87	        configDir: dirname(configPath),
    88	        config: null,
    89	        messages: [`${shortPath(configPath)}: <root>: Expected object`],
    90	      }
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    const result = OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema.safeParse(rawConfig)
    94	    return {
    95	      path: configPath,
    96	      configDir: dirname(configPath),
    97	      config: result.success ? result.data : parseConfigPartially(rawConfig),
    98	      messages: schemaMessages(configPath, rawConfig),
    99	    }
   100	  } catch (error) {
   101	    if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
   102	      throw error
   103	    }
   104	    return {
   105	      path: configPath,
   106	      configDir: dirname(configPath),
   107	      config: null,
   108	      messages: [`${shortPath(configPath)}: ${error.message}`],
   109	    }
   110	  }
   111	}
   112	
   113	function firstPath(layers: readonly LoadedConfigLayer[]): string | null {
   114	  const first = layers[0]
   115	  return first ? first.path : null
   116	}
   117	
   118	function firstFailingPath(layers: readonly LoadedConfigLayer[]): string | null {
   119	  for (const layer of layers) {
   120	    if (layer.messages.length > 0) return layer.path
   121	  }
   122	  return null
   123	}
   124	
   125	function mergeLoadedConfig(
   126	  userLayersNearestFirst: readonly LoadedConfigLayer[],
   127	  projectLayersNearestFirst: readonly LoadedConfigLayer[],
   128	): OhMyOpenCodeConfig {
   129	  let config = OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema.parse({})
   130	
   131	  for (const layer of [...userLayersNearestFirst].reverse()) {
   132	    if (layer.config) config = mergeConfigs(config, layer.config)
   133	  }
   134	
   135	  const userMcpEnvAllowlist = config.mcp_env_allowlist ?? []
   136	  for (const layer of [...projectLayersNearestFirst].reverse()) {
   137	    if (layer.config) config = mergeConfigs(config, layer.config)
   138	  }
   139	
   140	  config = { ...config, mcp_env_allowlist: userMcpEnvAllowlist }
   141	  return applyDisabledProviders(config)
   142	}
   143	
   144	export function validatePluginConfig(directory: string): PluginConfigValidation {
   145	  const userLayersNearestFirst = discoverUserLayers().map((layer) => parseLayerConfig(layer.path))
   146	  const projectLayersNearestFirst = discoverProjectLayersNearestFirst(directory).map((layer) => parseLayerConfig(layer.path))
   147	  const layers = [...userLayersNearestFirst, ...projectLayersNearestFirst]
   148	  const messages = layers.flatMap((layer) => layer.messages)
   149	
   150	  return {
   151	    valid: messages.length === 0,
   152	    messages,
   153	    path: firstFailingPath(layers) ?? firstPath(layers),
   154	    config: mergeLoadedConfig(userLayersNearestFirst, projectLayersNearestFirst),
   155	  }
   156	}

    13	type SolidRuntime<Node> = {
    14	  readonly createElement: (tag: string) => Node
    15	  readonly insert: (parent: Node, child: Node | string) => unknown
    16	  readonly setProp: (node: Node, name: string, value: unknown) => unknown
    17	}
    18	
    19	type SidebarSlotRegistration<Node> = {
    20	  readonly order: number
    21	  readonly slots: {
    22	    readonly sidebar_content: () => Node
    23	  }
    24	}
    25	
    26	type RegisterSidebarContentSlotInput<Node> = {
    27	  readonly registerSlot: (registration: SidebarSlotRegistration<Node>) => void
    28	  readonly requestRender: () => void
    29	  readonly renderSidebar: () => Node
    30	}
    31	
    32	function registerSidebarContentSlot<Node>({
    33	  registerSlot,
    34	  requestRender,
    35	  renderSidebar,
    36	}: RegisterSidebarContentSlotInput<Node>): void {
    37	  registerSlot({
    38	    order: 900,
    39	    slots: {
    40	      sidebar_content: renderSidebar,
    41	    },
    42	  })
    43	  requestRender()
    44	}
    45	
    46	function materialize<Node>(nodes: readonly ViewNode[], solid: SolidRuntime<Node>): Node {
    47	  const root = solid.createElement("box")
    48	  solid.setProp(root, "flexDirection", "column")
    49	  for (const node of nodes) {
    50	    solid.insert(root, materializeNode(node, solid))
    51	  }
    52	  return root
    53	}
    54	
    55	function materializeNode<Node>(node: ViewNode, solid: SolidRuntime<Node>): Node {
    56	  const element = solid.createElement(node.kind)
    57	  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(node.props)) {
    58	    solid.setProp(element, name, value)
    59	  }
    60	  if (node.kind === "text") {
    61	    solid.insert(element, node.text ?? "")
    62	  }
    63	  for (const child of node.children ?? []) {
    64	    solid.insert(element, materializeNode(child, solid))
    65	  }
    66	  return element
    67	}
    68	
    69	type RosterResolver = (directory: string) => RosterRow[]
    70	type PluginValidation = {
    71	  readonly valid: boolean
    72	  readonly messages: readonly string[]
    73	  readonly config: {
    74	    readonly tui?: {
    75	      readonly sidebar?: {
    76	        readonly enabled?: boolean
    77	      }
    78	    }
    79	  }
    80	}
    81	
    82	async function loadPluginValidation(directory: string): Promise<PluginValidation> {
    83	  const { validatePluginConfig } = await import("./config/validate")
    84	  return validatePluginConfig(directory)
    85	}
    86	
    87	async function loadRosterRows(directory: string): Promise<readonly RosterRow[]> {
    88	  const { resolveRoster } = await import("./features/tui-sidebar/roster-resolver")
    89	  const resolver: RosterResolver = resolveRoster
    90	  return resolver(directory)
    91	}
    92	
    93	async function readView(directory: string): Promise<SidebarView> {
    94	  const validation = await loadPluginValidation(directory)
    95	  const mirror = readMirror(directory)
    96	  const roster = await loadRosterRows(directory)
    97	  return computeView({
    98	    config: deriveConfig(validation),
    99	    roster: deriveRoster(roster),
   100	    agents: deriveAgents(mirror),
   101	    jobs: deriveJobBoard(mirror),
   102	    loop: deriveLoop(mirror),
   103	  })
   104	}
   105	
   106	export function handleTuiPollError(
   107	  error: unknown,
   108	  reportPollError: (error: Error) => void = (pollError) => log("[tui-sidebar] polling failed", { error: pollError }),
   109	): void {
   110	  if (error instanceof Error) {
   111	    reportPollError(error)
   112	    return
   113	  }
   114	  throw error
   115	}
   116	
   117	const module: TuiPluginModule = {
   118	  id: "oh-my-openagent:tui",
   119	  tui: async (api) => {
   120	    const solid = await import("@opentui/solid").catch(() => null)
   121	    if (!solid) {
   122	      return
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    const directory = api.state.path.directory
   126	    if ((await loadPluginValidation(directory)).config.tui?.sidebar?.enabled === false) {
   127	      return
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    let currentView = await readView(directory)
   131	    let currentKey = viewKey(currentView)
   132	    let disposed = false
   133	    let inFlight = false
   134	    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
   135	
   136	    registerSidebarContentSlot({
   137	      registerSlot: (registration) => {
   138	        api.slots.register(registration)
   139	      },
   140	      requestRender: () => {
   141	        api.renderer.requestRender()
   142	      },
   143	      renderSidebar: () => materialize(buildViewNodes(currentView, api.theme.current), solid),
   144	    })
   145	
   146	    const schedule = (): void => {
   147	      timer = setTimeout(tick, POLL_INTERVAL_MS)
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    const tick = async (): Promise<void> => {
   151	      if (disposed || inFlight) {
   152	        if (!disposed) schedule()
   153	        return
   154	      }
   155	      inFlight = true
   156	      try {
   157	        const nextView = await readView(directory)
   158	        const nextKey = viewKey(nextView)
   159	        if (nextKey !== currentKey) {
   160	          currentView = nextView
   161	          currentKey = nextKey
   162	          api.renderer.requestRender()
   163	        }
   164	      } catch (error) {
   165	        handleTuiPollError(error)
   166	      } finally {
   167	        inFlight = false
   168	        if (!disposed) schedule()
   169	      }
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    schedule()
   173	    api.lifecycle.onDispose(() => {
   174	      disposed = true
   175	      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
   176	    })
   177	  },
   178	}
   179	
   180	export default module

codex
The TUI read path uses `validatePluginConfig(directory)`, which does merge user and walked project layers and returns a config even when validation messages exist. I’m checking the runtime loader too, because the plan names both and they do not have identical failure behavior.

    19	function resolveHomeDirectory(): string {
    20	  return process.env.HOME ?? process.env.USERPROFILE ?? homedir();

    23	function resolveConfigPathAfterLegacyMigration(detectedPath: string): string {
    24	  if (!path.basename(detectedPath).startsWith(LEGACY_CONFIG_BASENAME)) {
    25	    return detectedPath;
    26	  }
    27	
    28	  const migrated = migrateLegacyConfigFile(detectedPath);
    29	  const canonicalPath = path.join(
    30	    path.dirname(detectedPath),
    31	    `${CONFIG_BASENAME}${path.extname(detectedPath)}`,
    32	  );
    33	
    34	  if (migrated || fs.existsSync(canonicalPath)) {
    35	    return canonicalPath;
    36	  }
    37	
    38	  return detectedPath;
    39	}

    41	function getUserConfigLayers(): Array<{ readonly configDir: string; readonly configPath: string | null }> {
    42	  const userConfigDirs = [...getOpenCodeConfigDirs({ binary: "opencode" })].reverse();
    43	  return userConfigDirs.map((configDir) => {
    44	    const detected = detectPluginConfigFile(configDir, {
    45	      basenames: [CONFIG_BASENAME],
    46	      legacyBasenames: [LEGACY_CONFIG_BASENAME],
    47	    });
    48	
    49	    if (detected.legacyPath) {
    50	      log("Canonical plugin config detected alongside legacy config. Remove the legacy file to avoid confusion.", {
    51	        canonicalPath: detected.path,
    52	        legacyPath: detected.legacyPath,
    53	      });
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    const configPath = detected.format !== "none"
    57	      ? resolveConfigPathAfterLegacyMigration(detected.path)
    58	      : null;
    59	
    60	    return { configDir, configPath };
    61	  });
    62	}

    64	function getCanonicalAncestorPathsNearestFirst(directory: string): string[] {
    65	  const homeDirectory = resolveHomeDirectory();
    66	  const stopDirectory = containsPath(homeDirectory, directory) ? homeDirectory : directory;
    67	  const ancestorConfigPathsNearestFirst = findProjectOpencodePluginConfigFiles(
    68	    directory,
    69	    stopDirectory,
    70	  );
    71	  log("Walked ancestor plugin configs", {
    72	    paths: ancestorConfigPathsNearestFirst,

    74	    stopDirectory,
    75	  });
    76	
    77	  return ancestorConfigPathsNearestFirst.map((ancestorPath) => {
    78	    const opencodeDir = path.dirname(ancestorPath);
    79	    const ancestorDetected = detectPluginConfigFile(opencodeDir, {
    80	      basenames: [CONFIG_BASENAME],
    81	      legacyBasenames: [LEGACY_CONFIG_BASENAME],
    82	    });
    83	    if (ancestorDetected.legacyPath) {
    84	      log("Canonical plugin config detected alongside legacy config. Remove the legacy file to avoid confusion.", {
    85	        canonicalPath: ancestorDetected.path,
    86	        legacyPath: ancestorDetected.legacyPath,
    87	      });
    88	    }
    89	    return resolveConfigPathAfterLegacyMigration(ancestorPath);
    90	  });
    91	}

    93	function resolveUserAgentDefinitions(
    94	  config: Partial<OhMyOpenCodeConfig>,
    95	  configDir: string,
    96	): void {
    97	  if (!config.agent_definitions) return;
    98	
    99	  config.agent_definitions = resolveAgentDefinitionPaths(
   100	    config.agent_definitions,
   101	    configDir,
   102	    null,
   103	  );
   104	}

   106	function resolveAncestorAgentDefinitions(
   107	  config: Partial<OhMyOpenCodeConfig>,
   108	  ancestorPath: string,
   109	): void {
   110	  if (!config.agent_definitions) return;
   111	
   112	  const ancestorBasePath = path.dirname(ancestorPath);
   113	  const ancestorDir = path.dirname(ancestorBasePath);
   114	  config.agent_definitions = resolveAgentDefinitionPaths(
   115	    config.agent_definitions,
   116	    ancestorBasePath,
   117	    ancestorDir,
   118	  );
   119	}

   121	export function loadPluginConfig(
   122	  directory: string,
   123	  ctx: unknown
   124	): OhMyOpenCodeConfig {
   125	  const userConfigLayers = getUserConfigLayers();
   126	  const canonicalAncestorPathsNearestFirst = getCanonicalAncestorPathsNearestFirst(directory);
   127	
   128	  let config: OhMyOpenCodeConfig = OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema.parse({});
   129	  let mergedUserGitMasterOverrides: Record<string, unknown> | null = null;
   130	
   131	  for (const userLayer of userConfigLayers) {
   132	    if (!userLayer.configPath) continue;
   133	
   134	    const userConfig = loadConfigFromPath(userLayer.configPath, ctx);
   135	    const userGitMasterOverrides = loadExplicitGitMasterOverrides(userLayer.configPath);
   136	
   137	    if (userConfig) {
   138	      resolveUserAgentDefinitions(userConfig, userLayer.configDir);
   139	      config = mergeConfigs(config, userConfig);
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    if (userGitMasterOverrides) {
   143	      mergedUserGitMasterOverrides = {
   144	        ...(mergedUserGitMasterOverrides ?? {}),
   145	        ...userGitMasterOverrides,
   146	      };
   147	    }
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  const userMcpEnvAllowlist = config.mcp_env_allowlist ?? [];
   151	  const canonicalAncestorPathsFarthestFirst = [...canonicalAncestorPathsNearestFirst].reverse();
   152	  const defaultGitMaster = OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema.parse({}).git_master;
   153	  const ancestorGitMasterOverridesFarthestFirst: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
   154	
   155	  for (const ancestorPath of canonicalAncestorPathsFarthestFirst) {
   156	    const ancestorConfig = loadConfigFromPath(ancestorPath, ctx);
   157	    const ancestorOverrides = loadExplicitGitMasterOverrides(ancestorPath);
   158	
   159	    if (ancestorConfig) {
   160	      resolveAncestorAgentDefinitions(ancestorConfig, ancestorPath);
   161	      config = mergeConfigs(config, ancestorConfig);
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    if (ancestorOverrides) {
   165	      ancestorGitMasterOverridesFarthestFirst.push(ancestorOverrides);
   166	    }
   167	  }
   168	
   169	  if (mergedUserGitMasterOverrides || ancestorGitMasterOverridesFarthestFirst.length > 0) {
   170	    const mergedAncestorGitMaster: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   171	    for (const override of ancestorGitMasterOverridesFarthestFirst) {
   172	      Object.assign(mergedAncestorGitMaster, override);
   173	    }
   174	    config = {
   175	      ...config,
   176	      git_master: {
   177	        ...defaultGitMaster,
   178	        ...(mergedUserGitMasterOverrides ?? {}),
   179	        ...mergedAncestorGitMaster,
   180	      },
   181	    };
   182	  }
   183	
   184	  config = {
   185	    ...config,
   186	    mcp_env_allowlist: userMcpEnvAllowlist,
   187	  };
   188	
   189	  applyDisabledProviders(config);
   190	
   191	  log("Final merged config", {
   192	    agents: config.agents,
   193	    team_mode: config.team_mode,
   194	    disabled_agents: config.disabled_agents,
   195	    disabled_mcps: config.disabled_mcps,
   196	    disabled_hooks: config.disabled_hooks,
   197	    disabled_providers: config.disabled_providers,
   198	    claude_code: config.claude_code,
   199	  });
   200	  return config;
   201	}

     6	  override: Partial<OhMyOpenCodeConfig>

    20	    disabled_tools: mergeUniqueStrings(base.disabled_tools, override.disabled_tools),
    21	    disabled_providers: mergeUniqueStringsCaseInsensitive(base.disabled_providers, override.disabled_providers),
    22	    mcp_env_allowlist: override.mcp_env_allowlist ?? base.mcp_env_allowlist,
    23	    claude_code: deepMerge(base.claude_code, override.claude_code),

     1	import { isPlainRecord, parseConfigSections } from "@oh-my-opencode/utils"
     2	import * as fs from "fs";
     3	import { OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema, type OhMyOpenCodeConfig } from "../config";
     4	import {
     5	  addConfigLoadError,
     6	  log,
     7	  migrateConfigFile,
     8	  parseJsonc,
     9	} from "../shared";
    10	import { addAgentOrderWarnings } from "./agent-order-warnings";
    11	
    12	
    13	
    14	export function loadExplicitGitMasterOverrides(configPath: string): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
    15	  try {
    16	    if (!fs.existsSync(configPath)) {
    17	      return undefined;
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    const content = fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf-8");
    21	    const rawConfig = parseJsonc<Record<string, unknown>>(content);
    22	    const gitMaster = rawConfig.git_master;
    23	
    24	    if (isPlainRecord(gitMaster)) {
    25	      return gitMaster;
    26	    }
    27	  } catch (error) {
    28	    if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
    29	      throw error;
    30	    }
    31	    return undefined;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  return undefined;
    35	}
    36	
    37	export function parseConfigPartially(
    38	  rawConfig: Record<string, unknown>
    39	): Partial<OhMyOpenCodeConfig> | null {
    40	  return parseConfigSections(OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema, rawConfig, {
    41	    onInvalidSections: (invalidSections) => {
    42	      log("Partial config loaded - invalid sections skipped:", invalidSections);
    43	    },
    44	  });
    45	}
    46	
    47	export function loadConfigFromPath(
    48	  configPath: string,
    49	  _ctx: unknown
    50	): Partial<OhMyOpenCodeConfig> | null {
    51	  try {
    52	    if (fs.existsSync(configPath)) {
    53	      const content = fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf-8");
    54	      const rawConfig = parseJsonc<Record<string, unknown>>(content);
    55	
    56	      migrateConfigFile(configPath, rawConfig);
    57	
    58	      const result = OhMyOpenCodeConfigSchema.safeParse(rawConfig);
    59	
    60	      if (result.success) {
    61	        addAgentOrderWarnings(configPath, result.data.agent_order);
    62	        log(`Config loaded from ${configPath}`, {
    63	          agents: result.data.agents,
    64	          team_mode: result.data.team_mode,
    65	        });
    66	        return result.data;
    67	      }
    68	
    69	      const errorMsg = result.error.issues
    70	        .map((i) => `${i.path.join(".")}: ${i.message}`)
    71	        .join(", ");
    72	      log(`Config validation error in ${configPath}:`, result.error.issues);
    73	      addConfigLoadError({
    74	        path: configPath,
    75	        error: `Partial config loaded - invalid sections skipped: ${errorMsg}`,
    76	      });
    77	
    78	      const partialResult = parseConfigPartially(rawConfig);
    79	      if (partialResult) {
    80	        addAgentOrderWarnings(configPath, partialResult.agent_order);
    81	        log(`Partial config loaded from ${configPath}`, {
    82	          agents: partialResult.agents,
    83	          team_mode: partialResult.team_mode,
    84	        });
    85	        return partialResult;
    86	      }
    87	
    88	      return null;
    89	    }
    90	  } catch (err) {
    91	    const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    92	    log(`Error loading config from ${configPath}:`, err);
    93	    addConfigLoadError({ path: configPath, error: errorMsg });
    94	  }
    95	  return null;
    96	}

     9	type EnvSnapshot = {
    10	  readonly HOME: string | undefined
    11	  readonly OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR: string | undefined
    12	  readonly XDG_CONFIG_HOME: string | undefined
    13	}
    14	
    15	const ENV_KEYS = ["HOME", "OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR", "XDG_CONFIG_HOME"] as const
    16	
    17	function restoreEnv(snapshot: EnvSnapshot): void {
    18	  for (const key of ENV_KEYS) {
    19	    const value = snapshot[key]
    20	    if (value === undefined) {
    21	      delete process.env[key]
    22	    } else {
    23	      process.env[key] = value
    24	    }
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	function withIsolatedConfig<T>(name: string, run: (root: string) => T): T {
    29	  const original: EnvSnapshot = {
    30	    HOME: process.env.HOME,
    31	    OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR: process.env.OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR,
    32	    XDG_CONFIG_HOME: process.env.XDG_CONFIG_HOME,
    33	  }
    34	  const root = join(tmpdir(), `omo-config-validate-${name}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`)
    35	
    36	  try {
    37	    mkdirSync(root, { recursive: true })
    38	    process.env.HOME = root
    39	    process.env.OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR = join(root, "custom-config")
    40	    process.env.XDG_CONFIG_HOME = join(root, "xdg-config")
    41	    return run(root)
    42	  } finally {
    43	    rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
    44	    restoreEnv(original)
    45	  }
    46	}
    47	
    48	function writeJson(filePath: string, value: unknown): void {
    49	  mkdirSync(join(filePath, ".."), { recursive: true })
    50	  writeFileSync(filePath, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`, "utf-8")
    51	}

    53	function snapshotFiles(directory: string): Record<string, number> {
    54	  const files: Record<string, number> = {}
    55	  for (const fileName of readdirSync(directory)) {
    56	    const filePath = join(directory, fileName)
    57	    files[fileName] = statSync(filePath).mtimeMs
    58	  }
    59	  return files
    60	}

codex
One loader distinction is important: `validatePluginConfig` is read-only and tolerant of invalid sections, while `loadPluginConfig` may migrate legacy config files and records load errors. For lazy reload inside a send/drain, the read-only loader is the safer match to the plan’s “no bespoke parse, no migration” intent.

    14	type ThemeLike = {
    15	  readonly error?: unknown
    16	  readonly text?: unknown
    17	  readonly textMuted?: unknown

    25	export function buildViewNodes(view: SidebarView, theme: ThemeLike): ViewNode[] {
    26	  switch (view.kind) {
    27	    case "active":
    28	      return [
    29	        box({ flexDirection: "column", gap: 1 }, [
    30	          ...configBannerNodes(view.configBanner, theme),
    31	          ...loopNodes(view.loop, theme),
    32	          ...agentNodes(view.agents, theme),
    33	          ...jobNodes(view.jobs, theme),
    34	        ]),
    35	      ]
    36	    case "broken":
    37	      return brokenNodes(view.messages, theme)
    38	    case "idle":
    39	      return idleNodes(view.roster, theme)
    40	    default:
    41	      return assertNever(view)
    42	  }
    43	}
    44	
    45	export function describeView(view: SidebarView): string {
    46	  return linesForView(view).join("\n")
    47	}
    48	
    49	function linesForView(view: SidebarView): string[] {
    50	  switch (view.kind) {
    51	    case "active":
    52	      return [
    53	        ...configBannerLines(view.configBanner),
    54	        ...loopLines(view.loop),
    55	        ...agentLines(view.agents),
    56	        ...jobLines(view.jobs),
    57	      ]
    58	    case "broken":
    59	      return ["config invalid - run doctor", ...view.messages]
    60	    case "idle":
    61	      return rosterLines(view.roster)
    62	    default:
    63	      return assertNever(view)
    64	  }
    65	}

    67	function configBannerNodes(banner: ConfigBanner, theme: ThemeLike): ViewNode[] {
    68	  switch (banner.kind) {
    69	    case "none":
    70	      return []
    71	    case "invalid":
    72	      return [text({ fg: theme.warning }, "config invalid - run doctor")]
    73	    default:
    74	      return assertNever(banner)
    75	  }
    76	}

    89	function loopNodes(loop: LoopState, theme: ThemeLike): ViewNode[] {
    90	  switch (loop.kind) {
    91	    case "none":
    92	      return []
    93	    case "live":
    94	      return [
    95	        section("ULW", theme, [
    96	          text({ fg: theme.text }, `goals ${loop.goalsDone}/${loop.goalsTotal}`),
    97	          text({ fg: theme.success }, `pass ${loop.pass}`),
    98	          text({ fg: theme.error }, `fail ${loop.fail}`),
    99	          text({ fg: theme.textMuted }, `pending ${loop.pending} blocked ${loop.blocked}`),
   100	          text({ fg: theme.accent }, `active ${truncate(activeGoalLabel(loop.activeGoal))}`),
   101	        ]),
   102	      ]
   103	    default:
   104	      return assertNever(loop)
   105	  }
   106	}

   109	  switch (loop.kind) {
   110	    case "none":

   174	  switch (jobs.kind) {
   175	    case "none":
   176	      return []
   177	    case "list":
   178	      return jobs.jobs.flatMap((job) => [
   179	        "Jobs",
   180	        `${job.title} ${job.status} calls ${job.toolCalls ?? 0} last ${job.lastTool ?? "none"}`,
   181	      ])
   182	    default:
   183	      return assertNever(jobs)
   184	  }
   185	}

   187	function brokenNodes(messages: readonly string[], theme: ThemeLike): ViewNode[] {
   188	  return [
   189	    section("Config", theme, [
   190	      text({ fg: theme.error }, "config invalid - run doctor"),

   195	
   196	function idleNodes(roster: RosterState, theme: ThemeLike): ViewNode[] {
   197	  return [section("Models", theme, rosterLines(roster).map((line) => text({ fg: theme.text }, line)))]
   198	}
   199	
   200	function rosterLines(roster: RosterState): string[] {
   201	  switch (roster.kind) {
   202	    case "empty":
   203	      return ["No configured models"]
   204	    case "rows":
   205	      return roster.rows.map((row) => `${row.label} ${row.model}`)
   206	    default:
   207	      return assertNever(roster)
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	function section(title: string, theme: ThemeLike, children: readonly ViewNode[]): ViewNode {
   212	  return box({ borderStyle: "single", borderColor: theme.borderSubtle, flexDirection: "column", padding: 1 }, [
   213	    text({ fg: theme.info }, title),
   214	    ...children,
   215	  ])
   216	}
   217	
   218	function truncate(value: string): string {
   219	  return value.length <= LABEL_MAX ? value : `${value.slice(0, LABEL_MAX - 3)}...`
   220	}
   221	
   222	function activeGoalLabel(activeGoal: string | null): string {
   223	  return activeGoal ?? "private"
   224	}

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba packages/omo-opencode/src/features/tui-sidebar/compute-view.ts' in /Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import type { MailboxSidebarState } from "../cross-project-mailbox/sidebar"
     2	import { assertNever } from "./state-types"
     3	import type {
     4	  AgentsState,
     5	  ConfigState,
     6	  JobBoardState,
     7	  LoopLive,
     8	  LoopState,
     9	  RosterState,
    10	  SidebarView,
    11	} from "./state-types"
    12	
    13	export type ComputeViewSections = {
    14	  readonly config: ConfigState
    15	  readonly roster: RosterState
    16	  readonly agents: AgentsState
    17	  readonly jobs: JobBoardState
    18	  readonly loop: LoopState
    19	  readonly mailbox?: MailboxSidebarState | null
    20	}
    21	
    22	export function computeView(sections: ComputeViewSections): SidebarView {
    23	  if (isActive(sections)) {
    24	    return {
    25	      kind: "active",
    26	      loop: sections.loop,
    27	      agents: sections.agents,
    28	      jobs: sections.jobs,
    29	      configBanner: sections.config.kind === "invalid" ? { kind: "invalid" } : { kind: "none" },
    30	      ...(sections.mailbox === undefined ? {} : { mailbox: sections.mailbox }),
    31	    }
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  if (sections.config.kind === "invalid") {
    35	    return { kind: "broken", messages: sections.config.messages }
    36	  }
    37	
    38	  return { kind: "idle", roster: sections.roster }
    39	}
    40	
    41	export function viewKey(view: SidebarView): string {
    42	  switch (view.kind) {
    43	    case "active":
    44	      return stableKey([
    45	        "active",
    46	        loopKeyParts(view.loop),
    47	        agentsKeyParts(view.agents),
    48	        jobsKeyParts(view.jobs),
    49	        ["configBanner", view.configBanner.kind],
    50	        ["mailbox", stableMailboxKey(view.mailbox ?? null)],
    51	      ])
    52	    case "broken":
    53	      return stableKey(["broken", [...view.messages]])
    54	    case "idle":
    55	      return stableKey(["idle", rosterKeyParts(view.roster)])
    56	    default:
    57	      return assertNever(view)
    58	  }
    59	}
    60	
    61	function isActive(sections: ComputeViewSections): boolean {
    62	  return sections.agents.kind === "list" || sections.jobs.kind === "list" || sections.loop.kind === "live"
    63	}
    64	
    65	function stableKey(parts: readonly unknown[]): string {
    66	  return JSON.stringify(parts)
    67	}
    68	
    69	function stableMailboxKey(mailbox: MailboxSidebarState | null): readonly unknown[] | null {
    70	  return mailbox ? [mailbox.inboundUnread, mailbox.inboundProcessed, mailbox.recentSentCount] : null
    71	}
    72	
    73	function rosterKeyParts(roster: RosterState): readonly unknown[] {
    74	  switch (roster.kind) {
    75	    case "empty":
    76	      return ["roster", "empty"]
    77	    case "rows":
    78	      return ["roster", "rows", roster.rows.map((row) => [row.label, row.model])]
    79	    default:
    80	      return assertNever(roster)
    81	  }
    82	}
    83	
    84	function agentsKeyParts(agents: AgentsState): readonly unknown[] {
    85	  switch (agents.kind) {
    86	    case "none":
    87	      return ["agents", "none"]
    88	    case "list":
    89	      return ["agents", "list", agents.agents.map((agent) => [agent.name, agent.status])]
    90	    default:
    91	      return assertNever(agents)
    92	  }
    93	}
    94	
    95	function jobsKeyParts(jobs: JobBoardState): readonly unknown[] {
    96	  switch (jobs.kind) {
    97	    case "none":
    98	      return ["jobs", "none"]
    99	    case "list":
   100	      return [
   101	        "jobs",
   102	        "list",
   103	        jobs.jobs.map((job) => [job.title, job.status, job.toolCalls, job.lastTool]),
   104	      ]
   105	    default:
   106	      return assertNever(jobs)
   107	  }
   108	}
   109	
   110	function loopKeyParts(loop: LoopState): readonly unknown[] {
   111	  switch (loop.kind) {
   112	    case "none":
   113	      return ["loop", "none"]
   114	    case "live":
   115	      return ["loop", "live", liveLoopKeyParts(loop)]
   116	    default:
   117	      return assertNever(loop)
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	function liveLoopKeyParts(loop: LoopLive): readonly unknown[] {
   122	  return [
   123	    loop.goalsDone,
   124	    loop.goalsTotal,
   125	    loop.pass,
   126	    loop.fail,
   127	    loop.pending,
   128	    loop.blocked,
   129	    loop.activeGoal,
   130	  ]
   131	}

    51	  it("#given the TUI plugin starts #when it registers the sidebar slot #then an initial render is requested immediately", async () => {
    52	    // given

    54	    const disposers: (() => void)[] = []

    56

    58	      createElement: (tag: string): SolidNode => ({ tag, props: {}, children: [] }),

    60	        parent.children.push(child)
    61	      },
    62	      setProp: (node: SolidNode, name: string, value: unknown): void => {
    63	        node.props[name] = value
    64	      },
    65	    }))
    66

    68	      state: { path: { directory: tempDir } },

    71	        register: (nextRegistration: TuiSlotPlugin): string => {
    72	          calls.push("register")
    73	          registration = nextRegistration
    74	          return "omo-sidebar-slot"
    75	        },
    76	      },
    77	      renderer: {
    78	        requestRender: (): void => {
    79	          calls.push("render")
    80	        },
    81	      },
    82	      lifecycle: {
    83	        signal: new AbortController().signal,
    84	        onDispose: (dispose: () => void): (() => void) => {

    86	          return () => undefined
    87	        },
    88	      },

    26	  filteredTools: ToolsRecord
    27	  taskSystemEnabled: boolean
    28	}
    29	
    30	export function createToolRegistry(args: {
    31	  ctx: PluginContext
    32	  pluginConfig: OhMyOpenCodeConfig
    33	  managers: Pick<Managers, "backgroundManager" | "tmuxSessionManager" | "skillMcpManager" | "modelFallbackControllerAccessor" | "monitorManager">
    34	  skillContext: SkillContext
    35	  availableCategories: AvailableCategory[]
    36	  interactiveBashEnabled?: boolean
    37	  toolFactories?: Partial<ToolRegistryFactories>
    38	}): ToolRegistryResult {
    39	  const {
    40	    ctx,
    41	    pluginConfig,
    42	    managers,
    43	    skillContext,
    44	    availableCategories,
    45	    interactiveBashEnabled = isInteractiveBashEnabled(),
    46	    toolFactories,
    47	  } = args
    48	  const factories: ToolRegistryFactories = {
    49	    ...defaultToolRegistryFactories,
    50	    ...toolFactories,
    51	  }
    52	  const taskSystemEnabled = getTaskSystemEnabled(pluginConfig)
    53	  const allTools = {
    54	    ...createCoreTools({
    55	      ctx,
    56	      pluginConfig,
    57	      managers,
    58	      skillContext,
    59	      availableCategories,
    60	      factories,
    61	    }),
    62	    ...(interactiveBashEnabled ? { interactive_bash: factories.interactive_bash } : {}),
    63	    ...createTeamModeToolsRecord({ pluginConfig, ctx, managers, factories }),
    64	    ...createMonitorToolsRecord({ pluginConfig, ctx, managers, factories }),
    65	    ...createTaskToolsRecord({ taskSystemEnabled, pluginConfig, ctx, factories }),
    66	    ...createHashlineToolsRecord({ pluginConfig, ctx, factories }),
    67	    ...createMailboxToolsRecord({ pluginConfig, ctx, factories }),
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  const allToolNames = Object.keys(allTools)
    71	  const teamToolCount = allToolNames.filter((toolName) => toolName.startsWith("team_")).length
    72	  log("[tool-registry] Built tool registry", {
    73	    totalTools: allToolNames.length,
    74	    teamModeEnabled: pluginConfig.team_mode?.enabled ?? false,
    75	    teamToolCount,
    76	  })
    77	
    78	  for (const toolDefinition of Object.values(allTools)) {
    79	    normalizeToolArgSchemas(toolDefinition)
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  const filteredTools: ToolsRecord = filterDisabledTools(allTools, pluginConfig.disabled_tools)
    83	
    84	  const maxTools = pluginConfig.experimental?.max_tools
    85	  if (maxTools) {
    86	    trimToolsToCap(filteredTools, maxTools)
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  return {
    90	    filteredTools,
    91	    taskSystemEnabled,
    92	  }
    93	}

    29	const FRONTMATTER_REGEX = /^---\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n---\r?\n([\s\S]*)$/
    30	
    31	export function serializeEnvelope(envelope: MailboxMessage, body: string): string {
    32	  if (Buffer.byteLength(body, "utf8") > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
    33	    throw new Error(`body exceeds ${MAX_BODY_BYTES} bytes`)
    34	  }
    35	
    36	  const validated = MailboxMessageSchema.parse(envelope)
    37	  const frontmatter = yaml.dump(validated, { schema: yaml.JSON_SCHEMA, sortKeys: false })
    38	  return `---\n${frontmatter}---\n${body}`
    39	}
    40	
    41	export function parseEnvelope(fileContent: string): { envelope: MailboxMessage; body: string } {
    42	  const match = fileContent.match(FRONTMATTER_REGEX)
    43	  if (!match) {
    44	    throw new Error("mailbox message missing YAML frontmatter")
    45	  }
    46	
    47	  const rawFrontmatter = yaml.load(match[1] ?? "", { schema: yaml.JSON_SCHEMA })
    48	  const envelope = MailboxMessageSchema.parse(rawFrontmatter)
    49	  return { envelope, body: match[2] ?? "" }
    50	}

     1	import { existsSync, realpathSync } from "node:fs"
     2	import { homedir } from "node:os"

    14	  OpenCodeBinaryType,
    15	  OpenCodeConfigDirOptions,
    16	  OpenCodeConfigPaths,
    17	} from "./opencode-config-dir-types"
    18	
    19	export const TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER = "ai.opencode.desktop"
    20	export const TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER_DEV = "ai.opencode.desktop.dev"
    21	
    22	export function isDevBuild(version: string | null | undefined): boolean {
    23	  if (!version) return false
    24	  return version.includes("-dev") || version.includes(".dev")
    25	}
    26	
    27	function getTauriConfigDir(identifier: string): string {
    28	  const platform = process.platform
    29	
    30	  switch (platform) {
    31	    case "darwin":
    32	      return join(homedir(), "Library", "Application Support", identifier)
    33	
    34	    case "win32": {
    35	      const appData = process.env.APPDATA || join(homedir(), "AppData", "Roaming")
    36	      return win32.join(appData, identifier)
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    case "linux":
    40	    default: {
    41	      const xdgConfig = process.env.XDG_CONFIG_HOME || join(homedir(), ".config")
    42	      return join(xdgConfig, identifier)
    43	    }
    44	  }
    45	}
    46	
    47	function resolveConfigPath(pathValue: string): string {
    48	  if (isWslEnvironment() && pathValue.startsWith("/")) {
    49	    return posix.normalize(pathValue)
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  const resolvedPath = resolve(pathValue)
    53	  if (!existsSync(resolvedPath)) return resolvedPath
    54	
    55	  try {
    56	    return realpathSync(resolvedPath)
    57	  } catch (error) {
    58	    if (error instanceof Error) {
    59	      return resolvedPath
    60	    }
    61	    return resolvedPath
    62	  }
    63	}
    64	
    65	function isWslEnvironment(): boolean {
    66	  return process.platform === "linux" &&
    67	    (Boolean(process.env.WSL_DISTRO_NAME?.trim()) || Boolean(process.env.WSL_INTEROP?.trim()))
    68	}
    69	
    70	function isWindowsUserConfigRoot(pathValue: string): boolean {
    71	  const normalizedPath = pathValue.replaceAll("\\", "/").toLowerCase()
    72	  return /^[a-z]:\/users\//.test(normalizedPath) || /^\/mnt\/[a-z]\/users\//.test(normalizedPath)
    73	}
    74	
    75	function getWindowsUserFromConfigRoot(pathValue: string): string | null {
    76	  const normalizedPath = pathValue.replaceAll("\\", "/")
    77	  const match = /^(?:[a-z]:|\/mnt\/[a-z])\/Users\/([^/]+)/i.exec(normalizedPath)
    78	  return match?.[1] ?? null
    79	}
    80	
    81	function getWslLinuxHomeDir(windowsConfigRoot?: string): string | null {
    82	  const envHome = process.env.HOME?.trim()
    83	  if (envHome && envHome.startsWith("/") && !isWindowsUserConfigRoot(envHome)) {
    84	    return envHome
    85	  }
    86	
    87	  const user = process.env.USER?.trim() || process.env.LOGNAME?.trim() ||
    88	    process.env.SUDO_USER?.trim() ||
    89	    (windowsConfigRoot ? getWindowsUserFromConfigRoot(windowsConfigRoot) : undefined)
    90	  return user ? posix.join("/home", user) : null
    91	}
    92	
    93	function getCliDefaultConfigDir(): string {
    94	  const envXdgConfig = process.env.XDG_CONFIG_HOME?.trim()
    95	  const shouldIgnoreWindowsXdg = envXdgConfig !== undefined && envXdgConfig.length > 0 &&
    96	    isWslEnvironment() && isWindowsUserConfigRoot(envXdgConfig)
    97	  const xdgConfig = shouldIgnoreWindowsXdg
    98	    ? posix.join(getWslLinuxHomeDir(envXdgConfig) ?? "/home", ".config")
    99	    : envXdgConfig || join(homedir(), ".config")
   100	  const configDir = isWslEnvironment() ? posix.join(xdgConfig, "opencode") : join(xdgConfig, "opencode")
   101	  return resolveConfigPath(configDir)
   102	}
   103	
   104	function getCliCustomConfigDir(): string | null {
   105	  const envConfigDir = process.env.OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR?.trim()
   106	  if (!envConfigDir) {
   107	    return null
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  return resolveConfigPath(envConfigDir)
   111	}
   112	
   113	function getCliConfigDir(): string {
   114	  return getCliCustomConfigDir() ?? getCliDefaultConfigDir()
   115	}
   116	
   117	export function getOpenCodeConfigDirs(options: OpenCodeConfigDirOptions): string[] {
   118	  if (options.binary !== "opencode") {
   119	    return [getOpenCodeConfigDir(options)]
   120	  }
   121	
   122	  const customConfigDir = getCliCustomConfigDir()
   123	
   124	  return Array.from(
   125	    new Set([
   126	      ...(customConfigDir ? [customConfigDir] : []),
   127	      getCliDefaultConfigDir(),
   128	    ]),
   129	  )
   130	}
   131	
   132	export function getOpenCodeConfigDir(options: OpenCodeConfigDirOptions): string {
   133	  const { binary, version, checkExisting = true } = options
   134	
   135	  if (binary === "opencode") {
   136	    return getCliConfigDir()
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  const identifier = isDevBuild(version) ? TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER_DEV : TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER
   140	  const tauriDirBase = getTauriConfigDir(identifier)
   141	  const tauriDir = process.platform === "win32"
   142	    ? (win32.isAbsolute(tauriDirBase) ? win32.normalize(tauriDirBase) : win32.resolve(tauriDirBase))
   143	    : resolveConfigPath(tauriDirBase)
   144	
   145	  if (checkExisting) {
   146	    const legacyDir = getCliConfigDir()
   147	    const legacyConfig = join(legacyDir, "opencode.json")
   148	    const legacyConfigC = join(legacyDir, "opencode.jsonc")
   149	
   150	    if (existsSync(legacyConfig) || existsSync(legacyConfigC)) {
   151	      return legacyDir
   152	    }
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  return tauriDir
   156	}
   157	
   158	export function getOpenCodeConfigPaths(options: OpenCodeConfigDirOptions): OpenCodeConfigPaths {
   159	  const configDir = getOpenCodeConfigDir(options)
   160	
   161	  return {
   162	    configDir,
   163	    configJson: join(configDir, "opencode.json"),
   164	    configJsonc: join(configDir, "opencode.jsonc"),
   165	    packageJson: join(configDir, "package.json"),
   166	    omoConfig: join(configDir, `${CONFIG_BASENAME}.json`),
   167	  }
   168	}
   169	
   170	export function detectExistingConfigDir(binary: OpenCodeBinaryType, version?: string | null): string | null {
   171	  const locations: string[] = []
   172	
   173	  const envConfigDir = process.env.OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR?.trim()
   174	  if (envConfigDir) {
   175	    locations.push(resolveConfigPath(envConfigDir))
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  if (binary === "opencode-desktop") {
   179	    const identifier = isDevBuild(version) ? TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER_DEV : TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER
   180	    locations.push(getTauriConfigDir(identifier))
   181	
   182	    if (isDevBuild(version)) {
   183	      locations.push(getTauriConfigDir(TAURI_APP_IDENTIFIER))
   184	    }
   185	  }
   186	
   187	  locations.push(getCliConfigDir())
   188	
   189	  for (const dir of locations) {
   190	    const configJson = join(dir, "opencode.json")
   191	    const configJsonc = join(dir, "opencode.jsonc")
   192	
   193	    if (existsSync(configJson) || existsSync(configJsonc)) {
   194	      return dir
   195	    }
   196	  }
   197	
   198	  return null
   199	}

    22	// mounted SidebarContent registers its refresh here.
    23	let activeRecompPollKick: (() => void) | null = null
    24	export function kickRecompProgressRefresh(): void {
    25	    activeRecompPollKick?.()
    26	}

    28	const SINGLE_BORDER = { type: "single" } as any
    29	const REFRESH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150

    31	export interface SidebarController {
    32	    prefs: () => MagicContextTuiPrefs
    33	    collapsed: () => boolean
    34	    toggleCollapsed: () => void
    35	}
    36	
    37	// The TUI may unmount and remount sidebar_content when the user switches views
    38	// (main -> subagent -> main). A remount re-runs the component body, so a signal
    39	// created inside the component would reset to its seed. The controller lives in
    40	// the slot-factory closure (plugin/process lifetime) and owns the durable
    41	// prefs/collapse signals plus the single shared file watcher, so collapse state
    42	// and live pref reloads survive remounts. No Solid effects/memos here — those
    43	// need an owner; the poll-interval effect stays inside the component.
    44	function createSidebarController(initialPrefs: MagicContextTuiPrefs): SidebarController {
    45	    const [prefs, setPrefs] = createSignal<MagicContextTuiPrefs>(initialPrefs)
    46	    const seedCollapsed =
    47	        initialPrefs.rememberCollapsed && initialPrefs.collapsed != null
    48	            ? initialPrefs.collapsed
    49	            : initialPrefs.startCollapsed
    50	    const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = createSignal(seedCollapsed)
    51	    let lastPersistedCollapsed: boolean | null = initialPrefs.collapsed
    52	    let lastApplied = JSON.stringify(initialPrefs)
    53	
    54	    // Watcher lives for the process lifetime — intentionally never disposed.
    55	    // Collapse echo guard: lastPersistedCollapsed advances only once our own
    56	    // write lands, so a watcher echo of the value we just wrote is rejected by
    57	    // the `!==` check and cannot revert a user click.
    58	    watchTuiPreferences(() => {
    59	        void (async () => {
    60	            const next = resolveMagicContextPrefs(await readTuiPreferencesFile())
    61	            const serialized = JSON.stringify(next)
    62	            if (serialized === lastApplied) return
    63	            lastApplied = serialized
    64	            setPrefs(next)
    65	            if (
    66	                next.rememberCollapsed &&
    67	                next.collapsed != null &&
    68	                next.collapsed !== lastPersistedCollapsed
    69	            ) {
    70	                lastPersistedCollapsed = next.collapsed
    71	                setCollapsed(next.collapsed)
    72	            }
    73	        })()
    74	    })
    75	
    76	    function toggleCollapsed() {
    77	        const next = !collapsed()
    78	        setCollapsed(next)
    79	        if (prefs().rememberCollapsed) {
    80	            void queueTuiPreferenceUpdate(PLUGIN_KEY, ["collapsed"], next).then(() => {
    81	                lastPersistedCollapsed = next
    82	            })
    83	        }
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    return { prefs, collapsed, toggleCollapsed }
    87	}

    89	function compactTokens(value: number): string {
    90	    if (value >= 1_000_000) return `${(value / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)}M`
    91	    if (value >= 1_000) return `${(value / 1_000).toFixed(0)}K`
    92	    return String(value)
    93	}
    94	
    95	function relativeTime(ms: number): string {
    96	    const diff = Date.now() - ms
    97	    if (diff < 60_000) return "just now"
    98	    if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)}m ago`
    99	    if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)}h ago`
   100	    return `${Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000)}d ago`
   101	}
   102	
   103	// Text progress bar, e.g. [██████░░░░] for the recomp/upgrade live indicator.
   104	function progressBar(fraction: number, width = 14): string {
   105	    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fraction))
   106	    const filled = Math.round(clamped * width)
   107	    return `[${"█".repeat(filled)}${"░".repeat(width - filled)}]`
   108	}

     1	import { readFileSync, watch } from "node:fs";
     2	import { mkdir, readFile, rename, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";

     7	// Shared preferences file for OpenCode TUI plugins. One top-level key per plugin
     8	// (short, non-integer-like name, e.g. "magic-context"). The file is OPTIONAL:
     9	// every reader falls back to defaults when it is missing or malformed.
    10	//
    11	// Cross-plugin convention (anthropic-auth / aft / magic-context all mirror it):
    12	//   - same file name + env override + lookup order,
    13	//   - byte-identical `computeEffectiveOrder` so the three sort consistently,
    14	//   - a coordinated default-order ladder (anthropic-auth 160, AFT 180, MC 200).
    15	//
    16	// MC uses `comment-json` (already a dep, Bun-safe) for the WRITE path — a full
    17	// parse → mutate-one-key → stringify round-trip that preserves comments and
    18	// sibling plugins' keys. (anthropic-auth uses jsonc-parser's surgical `modify`;
    19	// AFT and MC use comment-json. Both are interop-safe as long as a sibling key's
    20	// values AND comments survive — asserted by the interop test.)
    21	
    22	export const TUI_PREFS_FILE_ENV = "OPENCODE_TUI_PREFERENCES_FILE";
    23	const FILE_NAME = "tui-preferences.jsonc";
    24	
    25	export function getTuiPreferencesFile(): string {
    26	    const override = process.env[TUI_PREFS_FILE_ENV];
    27	    if (override) return override;
    28	    const configDir =
    29	        process.env.OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR ||
    30	        join(process.env.XDG_CONFIG_HOME || join(homedir(), ".config"), "opencode");
    31	    return join(configDir, FILE_NAME);
    32	}
    33	
    34	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    35	    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    36	}
    37	
    38	// Tolerant read: a missing file, parse error, or non-object root all resolve to
    39	// {} so the sidebar never crashes on hand-edited content. Never throws.
    40	export async function readTuiPreferencesFile(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    41	    try {
    42	        const raw = await readFile(getTuiPreferencesFile(), "utf8");
    43	        if (raw.trim() === "") return {};
    44	        const root: unknown = parse(raw);
    45	        return isRecord(root) ? (root as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
    46	    } catch {
    47	        return {};
    48	    }
    49	}
    50	
    51	// Synchronous tolerant read — used once at slot mount to seed the initial
    52	// collapse state and effective order WITHOUT a frame of async flicker (the
    53	// sidebar must render at its final width/collapse on the very first paint).
    54	// Same tolerance contract as the async reader. Never throws.
    55	export function readTuiPreferencesFileSync(): Record<string, unknown> {
    56	    try {
    57	        const raw = readFileSync(getTuiPreferencesFile(), "utf8");
    58	        if (raw.trim() === "") return {};
    59	        const root: unknown = parse(raw);
    60	        return isRecord(root) ? (root as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
    61	    } catch {
    62	        return {};
    63	    }
    64	}

    73	    rememberCollapsed: boolean;

    77	        label: string;

    91	    forceToTop: false,
    92	    order: DEFAULT_SLOT_ORDER,
    93	    startCollapsed: false,
    94	    rememberCollapsed: true,
    95	    collapsed: null,
    96	    header: { label: "Magic Context" },

    98	        historian: true,
    99	        memory: true,
   100	        status: true,
   101	        dreamer: true,
   102	        stats: true,
   103	    },
   104	};

   106	function bool(value: unknown, fallback: boolean): boolean {
   107	    return typeof value === "boolean" ? value : fallback;
   108	}
   109	
   110	function int(value: unknown, fallback: number, min: number, max: number): number {
   111	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) return fallback;
   112	    return Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(value), min), max);
   113	}
   114	
   115	function label(value: unknown, fallback: string, maxLength: number): string {
   116	    if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0) return fallback;
   117	    return value.slice(0, maxLength);
   118	}
   119	
   120	// Per-key validation: every value is independently clamped/defaulted so one bad
   121	// entry never poisons the rest. Never throws. A missing/non-object MC key →
   122	// full defaults clone.
   123	export function resolveMagicContextPrefs(root: Record<string, unknown>): MagicContextTuiPrefs {
   124	    const entry = root[PLUGIN_KEY];
   125	    if (!isRecord(entry)) return structuredClone(DEFAULT_PREFS);
   126	
   127	    const d = DEFAULT_PREFS;
   128	    const header = isRecord(entry.header) ? entry.header : {};
   129	    const sections = isRecord(entry.sections) ? entry.sections : {};
   130	
   131	    return {
   132	        forceToTop: bool(entry.forceToTop, d.forceToTop),
   133	        order: int(entry.order, d.order, -10000, 10000),
   134	        startCollapsed: bool(entry.startCollapsed, d.startCollapsed),
   135	        rememberCollapsed: bool(entry.rememberCollapsed, d.rememberCollapsed),
   136	        collapsed: typeof entry.collapsed === "boolean" ? entry.collapsed : null,
   137	        header: {
   138	            label: label(header.label, d.header.label, 24),
   139	        },
   140	        sections: {
   141	            historian: bool(sections.historian, d.sections.historian),
   142	            memory: bool(sections.memory, d.sections.memory),
   143	            status: bool(sections.status, d.sections.status),
   144	            dreamer: bool(sections.dreamer, d.sections.dreamer),
   145	            stats: bool(sections.stats, d.sections.stats),
   146	        },
   147	    };
   148	}
   149	
   150	const FORCE_TOP_BASE = -100000;
   151	
   152	// Shared forceToTop convention — MUST stay byte-identical across anthropic-auth,
   153	// AFT, and magic-context or the three sort inconsistently against each other.
   154	// Forced plugins sort below FORCE_TOP_BASE, ordered among themselves by their
   155	// top-level key's position in the file, so users reprioritize by reordering
   156	// keys. The user-facing `order` knob clamps to -10000..10000, strictly above the
   157	// forced band, so a manual order can never beat forceToTop. Host slots render
   158	// ascending by order; OpenCode's built-ins occupy 100-500.
   159	//
   160	// Key-naming requirement: plugin keys must be non-integer-like short names (e.g.
   161	// "magic-context"). JS object key iteration hoists integer-like keys ("0", "42")
   162	// ahead of string keys, which would skew the indexOf-based ordering of forced
   163	// plugins. The shared convention requires non-numeric names.
   164	export function computeEffectiveOrder(
   165	    root: Record<string, unknown>,
   166	    pluginKey: string,
   167	    defaultOrder: number,
   168	): number {
   169	    const entry = root[pluginKey];
   170	    if (!isRecord(entry)) return defaultOrder;
   171	    if (entry.forceToTop === true) {
   172	        return FORCE_TOP_BASE + Object.keys(root).indexOf(pluginKey);
   173	    }
   174	    return int(entry.order, defaultOrder, -10000, 10000);
   175	}
   176	
   177	const TEMPLATE = `// Shared preferences for OpenCode TUI plugins.
   178	// One top-level key per plugin (short name). See each plugin's README for its
   179	// supported settings. This file is safe to hand-edit; plugins update individual
   180	// keys and preserve the rest (values and comments).
   181	{}
   182	`;
   183	
   184	type JsonValue = string | number | boolean | null;
   185	
   186	// Set a nested path on a comment-json root, creating intermediate plain objects
   187	// as needed. Mutating an existing leaf preserves its comments; sibling keys are
   188	// untouched. Returns false when the path is blocked by a non-object value.
   189	function setDeep(root: Record<string, unknown>, path: string[], value: JsonValue): boolean {
   190	    let node: Record<string, unknown> = root;
   191	    for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i += 1) {
   192	        const key = path[i];
   193	        const child = node[key];
   194	        if (child === undefined || child === null) {
   195	            node[key] = {};
   196	        } else if (!isRecord(child)) {
   197	            return false;
   198	        }
   199	        node = node[key] as Record<string, unknown>;
   200	    }
   201	    node[path[path.length - 1]] = value;
   202	    return true;
   203	}
   204	
   205	async function writePreference(pluginKey: string, path: string[], value: JsonValue): Promise<void> {
   206	    const file = getTuiPreferencesFile();
   207	    await mkdir(dirname(file), { recursive: true });
   208	    let text: string;
   209	    try {
   210	        text = await readFile(file, "utf8");
   211	    } catch {
   212	        text = "";
   213	    }
   214	    if (text.trim() === "") text = TEMPLATE;
   215	
   216	    let root: unknown;
   217	    try {
   218	        root = parse(text);
   219	    } catch {
   220	        // The shared file is currently malformed. Skip the write rather than
   221	        // clobber sibling plugins' keys — the user fixes the file, persistence
   222	        // resumes. (Collapse just won't survive restart until then.)
   223	        return;
   224	    }
   225	    if (!isRecord(root)) root = {};
   226	    if (!setDeep(root as Record<string, unknown>, [pluginKey, ...path], value)) {
   227	        return;
   228	    }
   229	
   230	    const next = `${stringify(root, null, 2)}\n`;
   231	    const tmp = `${file}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
   232	    await writeFile(tmp, next, "utf8");
   233	    await rename(tmp, file);
   234	}
   235	
   236	let writeChain: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
   237	
   238	// Writes are serialized on a promise chain: each update re-reads the file,
   239	// applies a comment-preserving edit to one property, and replaces the file
   240	// atomically (temp + rename in the same directory — the only safe cross-process
   241	// swap). Best-effort by design; preferences are never worth crashing the TUI.
   242	export function queueTuiPreferenceUpdate(
   243	    pluginKey: string,
   244	    path: string[],
   245	    value: JsonValue,
   246	): Promise<void> {
   247	    writeChain = writeChain.then(() => writePreference(pluginKey, path, value)).catch(() => {});
   248	    return writeChain;
   249	}
   250	
   251	const WATCH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;
   252	
   253	// Watches the DIRECTORY, not the file: editors and our own atomic writes replace
   254	// the file via rename, which kills file-level watchers.
   255	//
   256	// Two-stage filtering: (1) a cheap filename pre-filter on the prefs name or our
   257	// `.tmp`; (2) inside the debounce, re-read and compare against last-seen content
   258	// — the authority. Some platforms (macOS FSEvents, some inotify backends)
   259	// misattribute a sibling rename to the real filename, so a name filter alone
   260	// still produces strays; the content compare is robust against that, coalesced

## Blockers

- T1’s root default is unsafe with the real layered loaders. Each config layer is parsed through the root schema before merge, then merged shallowly. A defaulted `cross_project_mailbox` on every parsed layer can overwrite an earlier explicit `enabled:false` from user config when a nearer project config omits the block. See [validate.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/config/validate.ts:93), [validate.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/config/validate.ts:129), [layered-config-loader.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin-config/layered-config-loader.ts:128), and the shallow spread in [config-merger.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin-config/config-merger.ts:8). The plan must apply the mailbox default after merging, or strip default-only mailbox blocks from per-layer parsed configs.

- T4 becomes unreachable after T1 as written. `createMailboxSessionHooks` only calls auto-provision when `config` is falsy, but T1 makes omitted config parse to a populated object. Changing only the stub means no discoverable stub is written on the normal omitted-config path. See [create-mailbox-session-hooks.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin/hooks/create-mailbox-session-hooks.ts:18) and [auto-provision.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/auto-provision.ts:7).

- T3’s preferences helper acceptance is impossible as written. The plan requires comment preservation but says fallback to JSON parse/stringify; OMO does not depend on `comment-json`, only `jsonc-parser`. JSON fallback will not preserve sibling comments in `tui-preferences.jsonc`. See deps in [packages/omo-opencode/package.json](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/package.json:31). Magic Context uses `comment-json` explicitly at [/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/magic-context/packages/plugin/src/shared/tui-preferences.ts:5](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/magic-context/packages/plugin/src/shared/tui-preferences.ts:5). Plan should either add the dependency or use `jsonc-parser` surgical edits.

- T6 misses a second `max_body_bytes` freeze. The tool input schema is built once from `deps.config.bounds.max_body_bytes` at [project-message-tool.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/send-tool/project-message-tool.ts:121), but serialization also hard-caps bodies at `MAX_BODY_BYTES = 32768` in [schema.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/envelope/schema.ts:6) and [schema.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/envelope/schema.ts:31). Since config has no max constraint at [config.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/config.ts:25), raising `max_body_bytes` cannot hot-reload correctly.

- T5’s bounded-window guardrail is underspecified. Current `readRecentSent` reads and parses the entire outbox log, then slices the last three entries at [mailbox-sidebar.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/sidebar/mailbox-sidebar.ts:67) and [mailbox-sidebar.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/sidebar/mailbox-sidebar.ts:72). The plan only requires bounding stat checks, not log read/parse, while also saying “NO unbounded outbox scan.”

## Non-blocking notes

- Ack semantics are correctly identified: `ack()` moves to `processed/`, `quarantine()` moves to `rejected/`, and `.delivering-*` files are excluded from unread counts. See [mailbox-store.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:38), [mailbox-store.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:116), and [mailbox-store.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:138).

- TUI model claims are accurate: `readView()` does not pass mailbox today, `computeView()` only carries mailbox on active views, `viewKey()` hashes only existing mailbox counts, and `materialize()` forwards function props via `setProp`. See [tui.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/tui.ts:97), [compute-view.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/tui-sidebar/compute-view.ts:22), [compute-view.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/tui-sidebar/compute-view.ts:69), and [tui.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/tui.ts:55).

- The merged loader exists and is callable by directory: `validatePluginConfig(directory)` is read-only and better aligned with lazy reload than runtime `loadPluginConfig`, which can migrate legacy files. Use `valid/messages` carefully, because parse failures can otherwise produce defaults instead of last-known-good.

- Additional tests to update are not limited to `config.test.ts`; the unset-hook behavior in [create-mailbox-session-hooks.test.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin/hooks/create-mailbox-session-hooks.test.ts:36) will need a deliberate new expectation.

Changing only the stub means no discoverable stub is written on the normal omitted-config path. See [create-mailbox-session-hooks.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin/hooks/create-mailbox-session-hooks.ts:18) and [auto-provision.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/auto-provision.ts:7).

- T3’s preferences helper acceptance is impossible as written. The plan requires comment preservation but says fallback to JSON parse/stringify; OMO does not depend on `comment-json`, only `jsonc-parser`. JSON fallback will not preserve sibling comments in `tui-preferences.jsonc`. See deps in [packages/omo-opencode/package.json](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/package.json:31). Magic Context uses `comment-json` explicitly at [/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/magic-context/packages/plugin/src/shared/tui-preferences.ts:5](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/magic-context/packages/plugin/src/shared/tui-preferences.ts:5). Plan should either add the dependency or use `jsonc-parser` surgical edits.

- T6 misses a second `max_body_bytes` freeze. The tool input schema is built once from `deps.config.bounds.max_body_bytes` at [project-message-tool.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/send-tool/project-message-tool.ts:121), but serialization also hard-caps bodies at `MAX_BODY_BYTES = 32768` in [schema.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/envelope/schema.ts:6) and [schema.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/envelope/schema.ts:31). Since config has no max constraint at [config.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/config.ts:25), raising `max_body_bytes` cannot hot-reload correctly.

- T5’s bounded-window guardrail is underspecified. Current `readRecentSent` reads and parses the entire outbox log, then slices the last three entries at [mailbox-sidebar.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/sidebar/mailbox-sidebar.ts:67) and [mailbox-sidebar.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/sidebar/mailbox-sidebar.ts:72). The plan only requires bounding stat checks, not log read/parse, while also saying “NO unbounded outbox scan.”

## Non-blocking notes

- Ack semantics are correctly identified: `ack()` moves to `processed/`, `quarantine()` moves to `rejected/`, and `.delivering-*` files are excluded from unread counts. See [mailbox-store.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:38), [mailbox-store.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:116), and [mailbox-store.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/cross-project-mailbox/mailbox/mailbox-store.ts:138).

- TUI model claims are accurate: `readView()` does not pass mailbox today, `computeView()` only carries mailbox on active views, `viewKey()` hashes only existing mailbox counts, and `materialize()` forwards function props via `setProp`. See [tui.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/tui.ts:97), [compute-view.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/tui-sidebar/compute-view.ts:22), [compute-view.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/features/tui-sidebar/compute-view.ts:69), and [tui.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/tui.ts:55).

- The merged loader exists and is callable by directory: `validatePluginConfig(directory)` is read-only and better aligned with lazy reload than runtime `loadPluginConfig`, which can migrate legacy files. Use `valid/messages` carefully, because parse failures can otherwise produce defaults instead of last-known-good.

- Additional tests to update are not limited to `config.test.ts`; the unset-hook behavior in [create-mailbox-session-hooks.test.ts](/Volumes/Topper2TB/Git/agent-harness/packages/omo-opencode/src/plugin/hooks/create-mailbox-session-hooks.test.ts:36) will need a deliberate new expectation.
